# Bash tests of build, bundled scripts and checks in TypeScript

*Sub-spec J2 of the `large`-tier ticket #1231, row J2 of the architect decomposition
(<https://github.com/crewrig/crewrig/issues/1231#issuecomment-5857477069>). Parent
spec: `specs/0215-shell-to-typescript-migration.md` (requirement 13, row J, step (e) of
requirement 8), under the release-branch regime of
`specs/0215-shell-to-typescript-migration.delta-04.md` (requirements 27-38): this
spec-PR and its implementation pull requests target `release/1231-ts-migration`,
"shipped" means merged there, not reached `main`, and the base ref wherever a protocol
names `main` is `origin/release/1231-ts-migration` (requirement 34). Depends on the
sub-specs whose scripts these suites exercise and which shipped green: G1a
(`specs/0250-component-build-core-typescript.md` and delta-01), G1b
(`specs/0254-extension-plugin-builders-typescript.md` and delta-01), G2
(`specs/0257-packaging-release-typescript.md` and its deltas), H
(`specs/0258-skill-extension-bundled-scripts-typescript.md` and its deltas), I1
(`specs/0259-governance-checks-typescript.md` and delta-01) and, for the suites of
requirement 22, I2 (`specs/0262-model-ci-tooling-typescript.md` and its deltas, ticket #1339), whose switch pull request is not merged at authoring time. The models are the sibling
rows J1a (`specs/0260-hook-ops-tests-typescript.md`) and J1b
(`specs/0263-setup-service-tests-typescript.md`). Siblings of the same wave: J1a (#1340),
J1b (#1341), J3 (#1342, `specs/0261-e2e-harness-probes-typescript.md`) and the final removal
J4 (#1344).*

## Intent

A maintainer who changes a component build, an extension builder, a packaging or release
script, a governance check, the model or CI tooling or the harness curator gets the same
regression coverage as before, from tests that run with `node --test` on Linux, macOS and
Windows, with no Bash test file left for these scripts. A reviewer can see, suite by suite,
which behaviour each old Bash assertion checked, which new test checks it now, and evidence
that a broken implementation which turned the old suite red turns the new suite red too.
The release goldens are regenerated by a TypeScript generator in two Linux images, and the
harness-curator proofs run against a scratch palace and a stub `gh`, never against the real
MemPalace or GitHub.

<!-- markdownlint-disable MD029 -->

## Requirements

### Scope and boundary

1. **The suites.** The Bash suites and generators listed in the *Suite sections* below SHALL be
   replaced by TypeScript suites that run under `node --test`, and each Bash file SHALL be
   deleted by the pull request that replaces it. The list is fixed by this requirement and by
   the grouping of the *Decision record*; its source is the set of Bash files under
   `scripts/tests/` and the three files named by the architect decomposition that remain at
   the base commit after the rows J1a, J1b and J3, minus the files of requirement 2.
2. **Boundary with the sibling rows.** The following are NOT in this row, and this row SHALL NOT
   edit them:

   | Item | Row | Reason |
   |---|---|---|
   | `test-setup-*`, `test-component-tier-resolution`, `test-install-*`, `test-antigravity-component-install`, `test-system-context-store`, `test-tls-delegation`, `test-mcp-daemon`, `test-chroma-*`, `test-repair-mempalace-http`, `test-rotate-mempalace-token`, `test-mempalace-*` | J1b (#1341) | Spec 0263 requirement 1. |
   | Suites of hooks, usage, worktree, sync and transcript | J1a (#1340) | Closed. |
   | `test-e2e-*`, `test-antigravity-discovery-probe`, `tests/e2e/**` | J3 (#1342) | End-to-end harness. |
   | `test-check-bash32-portability.sh`, `test-check-pipefail-grep.sh` and the library they test | J4 (#1344) | Shell-only conventions deleted with the shell (parent delta-01). |

   An item of the J1b table that is still on the release branch while its pull request is open
   is not touched here; a suite of that table that J1b leaves behind is reported to the
   coordinator, not taken silently.
3. **Ambiguous rows.** A suite whose script is owned by several rows is taken by this row when it
   exercises a build, release, governance, model or CI script, and the *Suite sections* record the
   reason. The items delegated by the hand-offs of #1343 (the harness-curator suite, the scaffolder
   suites whose `fzf` stub is dead weight since spec 0258 requirement 24) are in this row.

### What a migrated suite preserves

4. **Replacement per suite.** Every suite of requirement 1 SHALL have the replacement suite and the
   assertion mapping given in *Suite sections* below. Each replacement file SHALL stay under 300 lines,
   a shared helper lives under `scripts/tests/lib/` and every new file is TypeScript (parent requirements
   1-3 and 11). Where the *Suite sections* report that an existing `.test.ts` already covers a group, the
   mapping names that test and the PLAN verifies by grep that it asserts the exact string before the Bash
   group is dropped (the *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME* notes).
5. **No assertion is lost.** Every case and group of a Bash suite SHALL appear in the mapping of its suite
   exactly once, with the replacement test that checks it and the strings or values that the replacement
   SHALL keep verbatim. A replacement MAY assert more than the Bash suite did, and SHALL NOT assert less,
   except for the entries listed under *Changed or dropped assertions* of its suite section, each with its
   justification.
6. **Sourced-library suites.** A suite that sources `scripts/lib/common.sh` or another shell library and
   calls shell functions in-process falls under the sourced-library exception of parent requirement 13, read
   as in spec 0263 requirement 6: (a) where the TypeScript twin of the function has its own black-box suite
   on the release branch, the Bash group retires and the mapping names, per assertion, the substitute test;
   (b) where no such test exists, the group SHALL be ported to a TypeScript test that spawns `bash` and
   sources the library, in its own file, POSIX-only (`skip` on `win32` with a stated reason), carrying the
   header comment `Retires with scripts/lib/common.sh (row J4)`, and the J4 ledger (#1344) SHALL list it so
   that J4 deletes it in the same diff as the function. A replacement SHALL call the TypeScript entry, not a
   shim that J4 later deletes, whenever the behaviour is the same.
7. **Shell-only properties.** An assertion that checks a property only a shell file has (a `bash -e`
   contract, a `bash -n` syntax check, an `EXIT` trap that restores a mutated tracked file, a nested
   `node --test` wrapper) SHALL be removed with its Bash suite and listed under *Changed or dropped
   assertions* (parent requirement 13).
8. **Mutations on a copy.** A mutation case SHALL mutate a temporary copy of the implementation files, never
   a tracked file in place, SHALL fail, not skip, when the text it needs to mutate is absent, and a mutated
   copy SHALL contain only the files that git tracks and a real copy of `js-yaml` and `argparse`, never
   `node_modules` and `dist` picked up from the host. An oracle that mutates a shell implementation SHALL
   mutate its TypeScript twin as well.
9. **Non-vacuity.** A replacement that iterates over fixtures SHALL assert a floor on the number of fixtures
   it found. A prerequisite that the CI runner image provides (a real `jq`, `yq`, `python3`, `node:sqlite`)
   SHALL make the test fail when the environment variable `CI` is set and skip with a stated reason
   otherwise.
10. **Hermeticity.** A replacement SHALL run with a scratch `HOME` and `USERPROFILE`, SHALL delete `CI` and
    any proxy variable that it does not mean to test from the environment of a child process that runs a
    partial copy of the repository, SHALL stub `gh`, SHALL use a local bare git remote created under its own
    temporary directory for any push, and SHALL write nothing under the real `~/.claude`, `~/.gemini`,
    `~/.copilot` or `~/.crewrig`. No test SHALL create a real label, issue, scheduled job, CLI
    configuration or spec-id reference. A test or harness SHALL NOT delete, restore, move or rewrite a path
    that it did not create with `mkdtemp`, and SHALL NOT take such a path from `process.env`, `argv`, `HOME`,
    `HOMEBREW_PREFIX`, a `PATH` entry or a git remote URL without a containment check inside its own
    temporary directory (incident 2026-10-17, #1673). Each file that runs a builder, installer or scaffolder
    SHALL use the real-home guard (`scripts/tests/lib/real-home-guard.ts`) and the proofs of requirement 14
    check the real home directories afterwards.
11. **Entry under test.** A replacement SHALL run the same entry the Bash suite ran, the `.sh` shim where the
    Bash suite exercised the shim, and the TypeScript entry directly through `process.execPath` where the Bash
    suite only needed the behaviour. The shim SHALL stay covered by at least one test per script that this row's
    suites reach through it.
12. **Behaviour of the scripts is frozen.** No pull request of this row SHALL change the behaviour of a script
    under test. A defect found while porting a suite SHALL be reported to the coordinator and the replacement
    SHALL assert the current behaviour with a comment naming the report.

### Proof that coverage did not regress

13. **Mapping audit.** Each pull request SHALL carry, in its body, the count of Bash cases removed and the count
    of replacement tests that cover them, and the reviewer seat SHALL be able to reconcile the two from the
    mapping of this spec.
14. **Mutation proof.** For each suite, the *Mutation set* of its section lists the mutants of the implementation
    that the proof applies. The pull request that replaces a suite SHALL show, in its body, a table with one row per
    mutant: the Bash suite run at the base commit turns red, the replacement turns red, and the unmutated baseline
    is green for both. A mutant that the Bash suite caught and the replacement does not catch blocks the pull
    request. Mutants named in the sections are proposals: the PLAN confirms the exact text of each against the file.
15. **Two environments.** A replacement that spawns Git, `bash`, `jq`, `yq` or a Python interpreter SHALL be run in an
    Ubuntu 24.04 container that mirrors the CI runner (Bash 5.2, `jq` 1.7, Node 24, GNU `tar` and `gzip` on the
    hermetic path) in two images, one with Node under `/usr/local/bin` and one under
    `/opt/hostedtoolcache/node/<version>/x64/bin`, before the pull request opens, with `CI=true` reproduced for any
    CI-aware skip. A golden value that contains a host-specific string (a Node path, a timestamp, a random token, a
    port) SHALL be normalised, and a golden SHALL be generated in a Linux container, never on the macOS host,
    then regenerated in the second image with `diff -r` empty. The real home directories are checked after each proof.
16. **Release goldens.** The generator `scripts/tests/fixtures/release/generate-github-golden.sh` SHALL be replaced by a
    TypeScript generator whose output is byte-identical to the committed goldens in both images; a fixture that is a
    `.sh` or `.js` file or begins with `#!` SHALL be stored with a `.golden` suffix. No proof or generator SHALL run a
    real publish.
17. **Harness curator.** The harness-curator proofs SHALL run `curate.py` and `apply.py` against a scratch palace created
    under `mkdtemp` and a stub `gh` first on `PATH`, SHALL assert that no path outside their temporary directory was
    opened for writing, and SHALL never reach the real MemPalace or GitHub.

### Wiring and deletion

18. **Deletion and allowlist.** The pull request that replaces a suite SHALL delete the Bash file, remove its line from
    `ci/shell-allowlist.txt` and keep `check-test-wiring` and `check-test-strays` green (parent requirements 10 and 12).
    Fixtures that only the deleted suite read and that are tracked `.sh` or `.js` files SHALL be ported or stored with a
    `.golden` suffix so that the ratchet baseline does not grow.
19. **CI wiring.** Each CI job that ran a deleted suite SHALL run its replacement through `node --test`, inside the
    stray-scan wrapper wherever the Bash invocation had it, in the same file and under the same job id, and the path
    filters of the job SHALL name the replacement files. The new suites SHALL join the existing TypeScript jobs, on
    Linux and on `windows-latest` where they need no POSIX tool (parent requirement 17), and the generated files SHALL
    be regenerated from `ci/ci-capabilities.yml` with `bash scripts/build-ci.sh` from a fresh base rather than edited
    or merged by hunks.
20. **Windows.** A file that needs `bash`, a POSIX permission bit, a symbolic link or a Python wrapper SHALL skip on
    `win32` with a stated reason that the Windows job names. A Windows job SHALL NOT pick `System32\bash.exe`, SHALL
    resolve interpreters from the parent `PATH` before narrowing the child `PATH`, and SHALL split test files under 300
    lines. A POSIX golden needs a separator and root normaliser or a POSIX-only skip. Windows git rejects
    `GIT_CONFIG_SYSTEM=<os.devNull>`: use `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1`.
21. **Static reads.** Every file that names a deleted suite SHALL be retargeted in the pull request that deletes the
    suite (or, when the diff would exceed review size, in a preceding pull request), and the *Static reads and wiring*
    of each section lists them. The files that read a Bash suite as data include `build-components-fixture-closure.test.ts`
    (a new inline `js-yaml argparse` literal in a suite SHALL be registered in its `LITERALS`),
    `install-oracle-mutation.test.ts`, `fixtures/install/old-invocation-allowlist.txt` and the CI wiring tests.
    An edit to a file of a sibling row goes to the coordinator as an `escalation` unless the coordinator authorises
    it in writing. Edits made to J2 suites by other rows (cases R4t, P6, S12 and SC1/SC2 of `test-check-ci-parity.sh`,
    case i of `test-check-claude-agent-layout.sh`) SHALL be kept by the replacement.

### Delivery

22. **Oracle rule and I2.** The suites whose script belongs to row I2 (`test-check-{model-mappings,agent-profiles,ci-parity,spec-id-reserved}`,
    `test-build-ci`, `test-model-resolution`, `test-reserve-spec-id` and any other suite of that script family) SHALL migrate
    only after the I2 switch pull request is merged and #1339 is closed, in the last pull requests of the *Decision record*,
    and the coordinator SHALL be asked before they start. Every other suite MAY migrate before.
23. **Pull requests.** The implementation SHALL ship as the pull requests of the *Decision record* into the release branch.
    Every implementation branch but the last SHALL be named `test/1343-<slug>`, and the last SHALL flip this spec and its
    deltas to `status: implemented`. Each pull request SHALL be rebased on the release tip, SHALL have green CI including
    `ratchet`, `lint-typescript` and the Windows jobs, and SHALL get an `APPROVE` verdict from a cold reviewer seat before a
    merge is asked. #1343 is closed by hand right after the last merge.

24. **Plan checklist.** The PLAN SHALL list every `VERIFY AT PLAN TIME` marker of the *Suite sections* as a checklist item, assigned to the pull request that depends on it, and a pull request SHALL NOT open while an item assigned to it is unresolved.

### Suite sections

For each suite: the replacement files, the mapping from each Bash case or group to the replacement test, the assertions
changed or dropped, the mutation set, the platform and the wiring. Case labels are those of the Bash suite, so that
`git log -p` of the suite at the base commit remains the reference. Items marked *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME* are resolved in the
PLAN before the first pull request opens.

#### Build-component suites (pull request P1)

Common facts for the five suites below. None of them sources `scripts/lib/common.sh` or any shell library in-process (grep for `source`  and `.`  returned nothing), so the R13 sourced-library exception does NOT apply and no port is marked 'Retires with scripts/lib/common.sh (row J4)'. Every suite drives `scripts/build-components.sh`, which is a forwarding shim over `scripts/build-components.ts` (row G1a, spec 0250, shim header L1-L18), so the shim and the TypeScript entry are both mutation targets. Replacement tests run the TypeScript entry directly through `node scripts/build-components.ts`, with `REPO_DIR` pointing at a `mkdtemp` root (never the checkout), and keep ONE shim-path assertion per suite family (see `build-components-shim.test.ts`, already covering shim equivalence, so none is added here). Nested `node --test` is not needed except for the `test-artifact-build-install-scope.sh` groups (unset `NODE_TEST_CONTEXT` there is moot because the groups are imported, not spawned). Child env: delete `CI`, set `LC_ALL=C`. No fixture added by these suites is a `.sh` or `.js` file, so the ratchet is untouched; any fixture text that must look like a script takes the `.golden` suffix. None of the five stages `js-yaml argparse` (no `STAGED_NODE_PACKAGES` literal), because the build runs from the checkout's `scripts/` and `node_modules`, only `REPO_DIR` moves; therefore `build-components-fixture-closure.test.ts` LITERALS (L24-L75) gets NO entry for these suites. *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME*: that the TypeScript build resolves `js-yaml` from the script location and not from `REPO_DIR` (if it resolved from `REPO_DIR`, a REAL copy of `js-yaml` + `argparse` goes into the sandbox, never a symlink, and a LITERALS entry becomes necessary). Sandbox contents: `git ls-files`-derived copies only (no untracked `node_modules`, no `dist/`).

Shared helper (new, under 300 lines): `scripts/tests/lib/build-sandbox.ts` exporting `mkBuildRoot({ core?, library?, community?, config? })` (copies git-tracked sources, never symlinks, so Windows is not blocked by `ln -s`), `runBuild(root, args)` (spawns `node scripts/build-components.ts`, returns `{ status, stdout, stderr }`, env scrubbed as above), `readFrontmatter(file)` (via `scripts/lib/build-components/frontmatter.ts` or `js-yaml`, replacing every `yq` call; `yq` is no longer a prerequisite of these suites), and `seedSkill/seedAgent/writeConfig` ports of the Bash helpers. *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME*: reuse `scripts/tests/lib/build-fixture-tree.ts` (already used by `build-components-output.test.ts`) instead of a new file if its API covers `mkBuildRoot`.

##### `scripts/test-build-components.sh` (107 lines)

- **Replacement suite.** Cases (a), (b), (c) in file order: (a) L82-L89 valid `canonical_repo` with `--check`, (b) L91-L100 `file:///tmp/foo`, (c) L102-L111 `.../blob/main/foo.md`. Overlap check done (`ls scripts/tests`, `git grep malformed`): `build-components-cli-config.test.ts` L180 (`MALFORMED` constant), `build-components-entry-form.test.ts` L145, `build-components-shim.test.ts` L182-L186 and `build-components-hardening-printed.test.ts` L162-L168 already assert exit 1 + the `Error: canonical_repo in crewrig.config.toml is malformed` line for a malformed value. NOT confirmed covered: the file:// scheme variant specifically (b) with the URL echoed, the `/blob/` variant (c) as the friction #77 witness, and the valid-URL pass (a). *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME* by reading `build-components-cli-config.test.ts` around L180 for the actual values; add only the missing ones.

| replacement file | what it holds | est. lines |
|---|---|---|
| `scripts/tests/build-components-canonical-repo.test.ts` (new, only if the VERIFY finds a gap; else extend `build-components-cli-config.test.ts` if it stays under 300) | (a), (b), (c) against the TypeScript entry in a throwaway `REPO_DIR` whose `crewrig.config.toml` is rewritten per case | ~70 |
| `scripts/tests/build-components-shim.test.ts` (existing, untouched) | shim parity for the malformed case | 0 |

- **Assertion mapping.**

| label | behaviour asserted | replacement::test intent | MUST survive |
|---|---|---|---|
| (a) | `canonical_repo = "https://github.com/crewrig/crewrig"`, `--target all --check`: exit 0 | canonical-repo::valid URL accepted by `--check` | exit 0 (vacuity: the root must hold at least one core component so `--check` is not trivially clean) |
| (b) | `file:///tmp/foo`, plain run: exit exactly 1, stderr contains `malformed` and the literal URL | canonical-repo::file scheme rejected | exit == 1 (not just non-zero); `malformed`; URL echoed |
| (c) | `https://github.com/crewrig/crewrig/blob/main/foo.md`: exit 1, stderr contains `malformed` | canonical-repo::blob path rejected | exit == 1; `malformed` |

- **Changed or dropped assertions.** The in-place rewrite of the REAL `crewrig.config.toml` with a `trap` restore is DROPPED: it is a host-safety hazard (a killed run leaves the checkout config broken) and is replaced by a per-case throwaway root. The awk line rewrite becomes a template string. The `canonical_repo` key is always present in the repo config, so the `else printf >>` fallback branch (L40-L42) is dropped as dead. Nothing else dropped. Note an upgrade: the Bash suite never asserted the second stderr line (`Expected: https://<host>/<owner>/<repo> ...`); the new test asserts it (additive, stronger).
- **Mutation set.** Temp copies only; mutate `scripts/lib/build-components/config.ts` `validateCanonicalRepo` (L102-L110) and the `CANONICAL_REPO` regex: (1) make the early return unconditional (`return`) -> (b) and (c) red. (2) loosen the regex to accept `file://` -> (b) red only. (3) loosen the regex to accept a deeper path -> (c) red only. (4) change the thrown status 1 to 2 -> (b), (c) red (exit == 1). (5) change `malformed` to `invalid` in the message -> (b), (c) red. (6) stop echoing `repo` in the message -> (b) red. (7) reject the valid URL (negate the test) -> (a) red. (8) shim `scripts/build-components.sh`: swallow the exit status (`exit 0` at the end) -> the shim case of `build-components-shim.test.ts`, not these.
- **Platform.** All platforms (pure Node, no Bash, no symlink).
- **Static reads and wiring.** The suite lives at `scripts/test-build-components.sh` (NOT under `scripts/tests/`): `Taskfile.yml` L456 (`test-components`: `bash {{.REPO_DIR}}/scripts/test-build-components.sh`; re-point to `node --test scripts/tests/build-components-canonical-repo.test.ts`); `ci/ci-capabilities.yml` L63, L90, L120, L145 (command `bash scripts/test-build-components.sh`), L155, L170, L189, L212, L227, L246 (trigger paths / cache files lists); `ci/shell-allowlist.txt` L109; `.github/workflows/build.yml` L51, L84, L90, L93, L96, L111, L138, L141, L156, L183, L186 (path filters plus `--key-files` and `hashFiles` lists); `.gitlab-ci.yml` L41, L66, L67, L75, L101, L134, L148, L154, L168, L191, L205, L211, L225 (re-run `git grep -n 'scripts/test-build-components'` at plan time for the exact set); `README.md` L515 (tree listing "Self-test for build-components.sh"); `scripts/check-stray-scan-wiring.ts` L35 (comment: this suite sits OUTSIDE the registered stray-scan population, so removing it needs no registry edit); `docs/adr/0001-e2e-docker-images.md` L12 (historical, left as is); `scripts/tests/build-components-ci-wiring.test.ts` L58 (`BUILD_COMMAND` alternation `test-build-components\\.sh`: replace by the new test file name, or drop if the capability then no longer builds through a script); `specs/*` hits left as historical. Because the hash lists key the cache, the TypeScript file is ADDED to each `--key-files` and the `.sh` removed in the same diff.

##### `scripts/tests/test-assembly-verification.sh` (108 lines)

- **Replacement suite.** One build, then 13 existence assertions in two families. Overlap check done: `build-components-output.test.ts` L98 ("core goes into the project tree, every other tier into dist/&lt;tier&gt;") and L133-L142 already assert the core/non-core routing; `component-roots.test.ts` and `component-twins-conformance.test.ts` name the `crewrig-assembly-test` fixture family. NOT covered: the specific fixture `tests/fixtures/overlay/artifacts/community` built as tier `community`, for all four CLI directories incl. Antigravity (`.agents`) with the real `developer` skill and agent. *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME* by grepping `build-components-output.test.ts` for `.agents/agents` and `AGENT.md`.

| replacement file | what it holds | est. lines |
|---|---|---|
| `scripts/tests/build-assembly-routing.test.ts` (new) | one `before` build over a root holding the real `developer` skill and agent (copied, not symlinked), `crewrig.config.toml` and the fixture community overlay; tests per CLI directory | ~120 |
| `scripts/tests/lib/build-sandbox.ts` (new, shared, see above) | helpers | ~120 |

- **Assertion mapping.**

| label | behaviour asserted | replacement::test intent | MUST survive |
|---|---|---|---|
| core skill x 3 CLIs (L68-L72 loop) | `<root>/<.claude\|.gemini\|.github>/skills/developer/SKILL.md` exists | assembly::core skill lands in the project tree, per CLI | one assertion per CLI directory, file path exact |
| overlay skill x 3 CLIs | `dist/community/<cli>/skills/crewrig-assembly-test-skill/SKILL.md` exists; key is the frontmatter `name`, not the fixture directory `test-overlay-skill` | assembly::overlay skill staged under dist/community, per CLI | name-keyed path |
| overlay agent x 3 CLIs | `dist/community/<cli>/agents/crewrig-assembly-test-agent.md` exists (flat file) | assembly::overlay agent flat file, per CLI | flat `.md`, name-keyed |
| Antigravity core skill | `.agents/skills/developer/SKILL.md` | assembly::antigravity core skill | |
| Antigravity core agent | `.agents/agents/developer/AGENT.md` (directory form) | assembly::antigravity core agent directory | `AGENT.md` inside a directory |
| Antigravity overlay skill | `dist/community/.agents/skills/crewrig-assembly-test-skill/SKILL.md` | assembly::antigravity overlay skill staged | |
| build success | `set -e` aborts when the build exits non-zero; the Bash run omits `--check` (recursion note) | assembly::the build itself exits 0 (asserted explicitly, `status === 0`) | explicit exit assertion |

- **Changed or dropped assertions.** `ln -s` of the core component directories becomes a copy (Windows-safe, immune to a build that follows or refuses symlinks); the "slice, not the whole tree" intent (issue #1432) is kept by copying only `developer`. The aggregated `FAILURES` list becomes one `test` per CLI directory (finer failure localisation, same checks). Antigravity overlay AGENT is NOT asserted by the Bash suite (only the overlay skill); it is kept un-asserted to preserve the frozen contract, *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME* whether to add it as a strengthening. The `--check`-recursion comment is moot and dropped.
- **Mutation set.** Temp copies of `scripts/lib/build-components/*.ts`, run through a `CREWRIG_REPO_UNDER_TEST` root: (1) `output-dirs.ts` route non-core tiers into the project tree instead of `dist/&lt;tier&gt;` -> every overlay assertion and the core-not-in-dist family. (2) `emit-skills.ts` key the output directory on the source directory name instead of the frontmatter `name` -> overlay skill x 3 (fixture dir is `test-overlay-skill`). (3) `emit-agents.ts` write the overlay agent as a directory -> overlay agent x 3. (4) `emit-agent-antigravity.ts` write `.agents/agents/<name>.md` flat -> Antigravity core agent. (5) `tiers.ts` skip the `community` tier -> all overlay assertions. (6) `staging.ts` do not stage `.agents` -> Antigravity overlay skill. #### Build-script suites (pull requests P1 and P3)

Common to the six suites below: each runs a `.sh` that is a forwarding shim over a TypeScript entry (or the shipped build output), so the replacement drives the TypeScript entry directly (`node scripts/<entry>.ts`) and, where the Bash case proves the shim, one smoke through the `.sh` shim stays in the shim-contract tests of the owning row (not re-implemented here). Sandboxes copy only git-tracked files (`git ls-files`) plus a REAL (`cpSync`, `dereference: true`) copy of `js-yaml` and `argparse`; nested `node --test` children unset `NODE_TEST_CONTEXT`; the child env deletes `CI`; any shell-versus-TypeScript comparison runs under `LC_ALL=C`, stderr included; fixtures that are `.sh`/`.js` carry the `.golden` suffix so the ratchet does not count them.

- **Replacement suite.** Overlap check done (`ls scripts/tests`, grep): the TypeScript units already cover the INTERNALS of every arm, never the sandboxed process-level scenario: `extension-check.test.ts` (150 lines: COMMITTED, RENDER-FAIL, VERSION-DRIFT, GAP arms and plugin-staging cleanup; closest to cases 6, 8, 15), `extension-check-scan.test.ts` (69: name-axis prefix rule, near-miss, one leading dot; cases 6e/6f internals), `extension-gap-record.test.ts` (93: `gapKey`, case 17d internals), `extension-context.test.ts` (240) and `extension-context-declared.test.ts` (90: span rules and diagnostics of case 19), `extension-shape-guard.test.ts` (99: case 9b message), `extension-validate.test.ts` (219: cases 5b, 18), `extension-cli.test.ts` (145: grammar, build mode, check mode, staging removal), `extension-render-gemini.test.ts` (163: case 1-3 internals), `extension-golden.test.ts` (139: `--target all` tree equality, non-vacuous both ways), `extension-conformance.test.ts` (167: shell/TypeScript conformance of the example extensions), `create-extension-differential.test.ts` (191: scaffolder, all 64 masks, shell vs entry), `extension-hook-static-reads.test.ts` (169, reads the `# BEGIN hook-file-twin` block of THIS suite, L613-623; see wiring), `extension-shim.test.ts` (108, shims). NOT covered end to end by any of them, hence the new files: the named scenarios of cases 5, 6b-6f, 7, 8, 9, 10, 11, 12-14, 16, 17, 18, 19 run through the real entry in a sandbox, the python-`tomllib` validity of the rendered `.toml` (cases 2-3), the real `extension-skeleton/` scan (11ii) and the case 4 non-empty Claude `SKILL.md` body. Per requirement 13 of this spec the replacement keeps the scenario level (process spawn on the sandbox) and does not re-test the units above.

| replacement file | what it holds | est. lines |
|---|---|---|
| `scripts/tests/lib/extension-sandbox.ts` (new helper) | `makeSandbox()` (copy of the git-tracked `scripts/` and `extension-skeleton/` only, REAL `fs.cpSync(..., {dereference:true})` copy of the production closure of `js-yaml argparse` computed as `build-components-fixture-closure.test.ts` does, empty `.git`, root `package.json`, `extensions/{core,library,org}`), `runEntry(sandbox, args, env)` (spawns the `.ts` entry through `node`, deletes `CI` and `NODE_TEST_CONTEXT` from the child env, forces `LC_ALL=C`, returns `{status, stdout, stderr, combined}`), `writeManifest`, `writeFiles`, `tomlParses` | ~90 |
| `scripts/tests/build-extension-suite-render.test.ts` (new) | Cases 1, 2a-c, 3, 4, 5a-d, 15a-b | ~230 |
| `scripts/tests/build-extension-suite-check.test.ts` (new) | Cases 6a, 6b (all seven), 6c-f, 7, 9a-c, 10a-b, 11(i), 11(ii) | ~270 |
| `scripts/tests/build-extension-suite-hooks.test.ts` (new) | Cases 8a-c, 17a-d, 18 (four CLIs, committed, matcher) | ~210 |
| `scripts/tests/build-extension-suite-scaffold.test.ts` (new) | Cases 12 negative control, 13 (two), 14, 16, with `hookFilePresent` | ~190 |
| `scripts/tests/build-extension-suite-context.test.ts` (new) | Case 19 in full | ~240 |

  Each file is < 300 lines. The five files share the helper; each `describe` gets its own sandbox factory call so a failing file cannot poison another (no shared `TMP_ROOT` state). The Bash suite shares one sandbox across the sub-cases of a case (e.g. 5a-5d, 6a-6f, 8a-8c, 17a-17d, 19): the TypeScript tests keep that sharing (sequential `test`s in one `describe` with a `before` sandbox) because the cases are cumulative by design (each step mutates the previous fixture state). Do not parallelise within a `describe`.

- **Changed or dropped assertions.** (1) The `fzf` stub and `PATH` prepend of case 12 are DEAD WEIGHT: the TypeScript scaffolder has its own prompt (`scripts/lib/create-extension/prompt.ts`, spec 0258 R24); the scaffold tests drive it through that prompt's non-interactive selection input (`VERIFY AT PLAN TIME` the exact input channel: stdin lines vs an env selection), never through a stub binary on `PATH`. The negative control keeps its meaning (a selection no skeleton directory offers prints no `Added:`). (2) The sed mutation of `scripts/lib/extension-hooks.sh` in case 17d is dropped: the builders read `hooks-vocab.ts` only, so mutating the `.sh` twin is unobservable (the Bash suite already guards the TS file with `-f`). The `.sh` mutation is not retained. (3) The `python3 >= 3.11` hard prerequisite (L35-43, exit 2) becomes `{ skip: "python3 >= 3.11 required for the tomllib validity check" }` on cases 2a and 3 only, with a sentinel test that fails under `CI` when python is absent (these two are the only independent TOML validity oracle; no TOML parser is a repository dependency, `VERIFY AT PLAN TIME` that none is in `package-lock.json`). (4) Case 1 writes to and removes `$REPO_DIR/build`: DROPPED, the replacement renders into a sandbox (host-safety: the checkout is read-only). (5) `NOTE: not found under node_modules` (L69-71, tolerated absence of `js-yaml`) becomes a hard precondition of the helper (`assert` with the dependency-install message): the TypeScript entry cannot run without it, and silent staging without it would yield a wall of unrelated failures. (6) `cp -r "$SCRIPT_DIR"` copies the whole working tree of `scripts/` including untracked and `node_modules` artefacts: replaced by a copy of `git ls-files scripts extension-skeleton` plus the explicit dependency closure (pitfall: sandboxes copy only tracked files). (7) Output comparison for Bash-sourced assertions runs under `LC_ALL=C`, stderr included (`2>&1` merge becomes `stdout + stderr` kept separate and compared in the shell-vs-entry leg only). (8) The `$(...)` stripped trailing newlines of case 19 content checks are made explicit (see the splice row). (9) `base64` round-tripping of captured output is dropped (a Bash artefact). Nothing else dropped; each of the 70-odd assertions above appears once.
- **Mutation set.** All mutants are applied to temp copies inside the sandbox (never to the checkout); each is tied to the case that must go red.
  1. `scripts/lib/extension/hooks-vocab.ts`: remove the `antigravity: { UserPromptSubmit ... }` gap so the event maps (gap disappears) -> Case 8a (observed record missing) and 8b.
  2. same file, set `antigravity: {}` for `PreToolUse` is the test's OWN mutation (17d): the mutant under test is `scripts/lib/extension/gap-record.ts` `gapKey` ignoring `event` (keying on `subject@target` + `hook` only) -> Case 17d (the second hook is silently satisfied).
  3. `scripts/lib/extension/check-arms.ts`: drop the `GAP-STALE` arm -> Cases 7 and 17c.
  4. same file, drop the `GAP-UNDECLARED` arm -> Cases 8b, 17b, 17d.
  5. `scripts/lib/extension/check-scan.ts`: `nameAxisMatches` from prefix to substring match (`includes`) -> Case 6e (`regeminate.md` charged); to exact-name match -> Cases 6c, 6d, 6f.
  6. `scripts/lib/extension-targets.json` read replaced by a hard-coded four-name list in `check-scan.ts` -> Case 6f only.
  7. `scripts/lib/extension-generated-class.json`: delete `claude-extension.json` from `manifest_class` -> Case 6a; delete `rules/AGENTS.md` -> Case 6b rules; add `README.md` (an `extension-skeleton/` file) -> Case 11(ii).
  8. `scripts/lib/extension/shape-guard.ts`: remove the `migrate-extension.sh` text from the message -> Cases 9b, 9c.
  9. `scripts/lib/extension/plugin-delegate.ts` (or the `--check` branch of `build-main.ts`): skip the post-check removal of `dist-*-plugin` -> Case 15a; sweep on every render -> Case 15b.
  10. `scripts/lib/extension/validate-percli.ts`: stop rejecting `hooks` under one CLI key -> Case 18 for that CLI only (proves per-CLI rows); `validate-hooks.ts`: accept `matcher` on `UserPromptSubmit` -> Case 18 matcher.
  11. `scripts/lib/extension/context-spans.ts`: remove the splice join (keep the newline) -> Case 19 splice; stop swallowing the interior line -> consumed-line; keep a blank line for a kept alone span -> kept-alone; drop the `$$` escape handling -> R4 passthrough.
  12. `scripts/lib/extension/context-refs.ts`: report only the first unresolved reference -> Case 19 growth; write output before validating -> Case 19 R5 absence leg.
  13. `scripts/lib/extension/validate.ts` (or the `extensionRoot` rewrite site in `gemini-render.ts`): forward `${extensionRoot}` unrewritten -> Case 5a; rewrite a bare form -> Case 5c; leave an empty `mcpServers: {}` -> Case 5d.
  14. `scripts/lib/extension/gemini-render.ts`: drop the `crewrig-provenance` line -> Case 2b; emit it inside the `prompt` -> Case 2c; emit provenance on a no-provenance command -> Case 3.
  15. `scripts/lib/extension/plugin-claude.ts`: leave the SKILL.md body empty -> Case 4.
  16. `extension-skeleton/base/` (sandbox copy): add a tracked `GEMINI.md` -> Case 11(ii); `scripts/lib/create-extension/` (scaffolder): stop deleting `hooks.json.fragment` after the merge, or scaffold `hooks/logger.sh` beside the `.ts` -> Case 16; write `gemini-extension.json` in the scaffold -> Cases 13 and 14; copy `commands/sample.toml` -> Case 14.
  17. Shim leg: `scripts/build-extension.sh` replaced in the sandbox by a copy that drops `exec` arguments -> every case (all depend on the shim path only if the test spawns the `.sh`). DECISION: the new files spawn the `.ts` entry directly through `node`, because the shim is covered by `extension-shim.test.ts`; only the Case 4/9c/12-16 sibling calls keep going through `build-claude-plugin.ts`/`create-extension.ts` the same way, so no shim mutant is needed here.
  Mutants 1-16 target the TypeScript twin as the implementation under test, as the Bash suite does (the shim forwards to it). `VERIFY AT PLAN TIME` the exact module and line of each mutation site (file names above are the `scripts/lib/extension/*.ts` modules; line numbers are read in the PLAN, not here).
- **Platform.** POSIX-only for the Case 1 Bash-oracle leg (spawns `bash`, sources `render-command.sh`) and for nothing else the suite needs. The remaining files are ALL PLATFORMS in principle (they spawn `node` on `.ts` entries in a sandbox), but the 11(ii) glob scan and the `fs.cpSync` dereference of the dependency closure must be proven on win32 (`VERIFY AT PLAN TIME`); the safe default is `skip` on win32 for `build-extension-suite-scaffold.test.ts` (scaffolder prompt and `hooks/logger.ts` are covered on Windows by `windows-create-extension.test.ts` and `windows-extension-hooks.test.ts`) and all platforms for the other four. The Bash-oracle leg `Retires with scripts/lib/render-command.sh` (the R13 sourced-library exception, bash-spawning port, row G1a/J4).
- **Static reads and wiring.**
  - `.github/workflows/build.yml` L1054 (path filter `'scripts/tests/test-build-extension.sh'`), L1123 (`run: bash scripts/ci-cache-guard.sh --stray-scan -- bash scripts/tests/test-build-extension.sh`).

| G6 (b) | no target output carries a `mempalace` server after that failure (a file absent is fine) | checks::failed render leaves no reserved server | probes `.mcpServers.mempalace` in the four paths; fix the Bash probe (it indexed `.mcpServers.mempalace // (...has(...))`, which is truthy for a present empty object only by luck: assert `has`) |
| G7 (x4, per command) | every `command` in each target's hook file, root token replaced (`${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}`, `${COPILOT_PLUGIN_ROOT}`, none for Antigravity working-directory form, `${extensionPath}`), names a path that exists; and at least one command was found | checks::emitted hook commands resolve | CHANGED, see below: the Bash extractor matches only `*.sh`, the example hooks are `.ts` since spec 0258, so it finds no path, `continue`s, and the only live assertion is the `checked > 0` one |
| G8 Claude | omitted matcher renders as the empty string | checks::match-all claude | `matcher == ""` exactly (not null: `jq -r` prints `null` for a missing key, so the Bash check is already strict) |
| G8 Gemini | `.hooks.BeforeTool[0].matcher == ".*"` | checks::match-all gemini | literal `.*` |
| G8 Copilot | `.hooks.preToolUse[0].matcher == ".*"` | checks::match-all copilot | literal |
| G8 Antigravity | `.["matchall-hooks"].PreToolUse[0].matcher == ".*"` | checks::match-all antigravity | key `matchall-hooks` |

- **Changed or dropped assertions.** (1) G7 resolvability is vacuous in the Bash suite today (extractor `\.(sh)$`; the emitted commands end `.ts`), so the only assertion that can fail is "no command found". The replacement extracts the first token ending `.sh` or `.ts` and asserts the file exists; it is an INTENTIONAL strengthening, and it must be run once against the frozen scripts to confirm it passes before it is committed (*VERIFY AT PLAN TIME*: if a command is `node <path>.ts` with the root token in the middle, the extractor must handle it; if resolution fails on the frozen output the test records a finding for a `tech`-class spec rather than relaxing). (2) `jq` becomes `JSON.parse`; `yq` is not used here. (3) `read -r -d ''` heredocs become TS arrays; the `# BEGIN/END hook-file-states` Bash marker block and the sed-driven extraction in `extension-hook-static-reads.test.ts` disappear (that test then imports `hookFileSetsMatch` directly). (4) The `matchall` sandbox in the Bash suite copies the LIVE `$REPO_DIR/scripts` (L440, not the tracked-only sandbox) and ignores the render exit status (`>/dev/null 2>&1`): the replacement uses a tracked-only sandbox and asserts the render exits 0 first (a failed render otherwise makes the four matcher reads `null`, a distinct, clearer failure). (5) The `GENERATED_CLASS` glob `case` is replaced by a RegExp conversion. (6) The Bash header's "no node/tsc runtime needed (CI capability declares tools: jq, yq only)" becomes false by construction: the capability must declare Node (it already does for the other `extension-builders-ts` tests). Nothing else dropped.
- **Mutation set.** Mutate the sandbox copy only. (1) `scripts/lib/extension/plugin-claude.ts`: stop copying `dist/` -> G2 Claude; same in `plugin-copilot.ts`, `plugin-antigravity.ts` -> G2 Copilot/Antigravity. (2) `scripts/lib/extension/plugin-common.ts`: also copy `README.md` into every plugin -> G2 (three). (3) `scripts/lib/extension/gemini-render.ts`: stop emitting `GEMINI.md` -> G3. (4) `scripts/lib/extension-generated-class.json` (sandbox): drop `hooks/hooks.json` from the class -> G3 (and the real `--check` arm c via `extension-check.test.ts`). (5) `scripts/lib/extension/mcp-delivery.ts`: emit `{}` instead of the declared servers for ONE target -> G4 for that target only. (6) same file: do not rewrite `${extensionRoot}` and leave the sandbox path in `.mcp.json` -> G5. (7) `scripts/lib/extension/validate-mcp.ts` L89-95 reserved-name set emptied -> G6a (the in-process `extension-validate.test.ts` passes a literal set so does NOT catch the production wiring). (8) `scripts/lib/extension/build-main.ts`: emit outputs before validating -> G6b. (9) `scripts/lib/extension/hooks-emit.ts`: root-token substitution for Copilot to `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}` -> G7 (and only the strengthened extractor catches it). (10) same file: Gemini match-all `.*` -> ` `-> G8 Gemini; Claude match-all` "" `->` .* `-> G8 Claude; Antigravity key` matchall-hooks `->` hooks `-> G8 Antigravity. (11) Rename a shipped hook handler` shell-logger.ts `->` shell-logger.sh `in the sandbox` hello-world `manifest -> G2 vacuity guard (and the static-reads matrix). Line numbers other than those cited are *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME* (`grep -n` the quoted token).
- **Platform.** Helper and both test files: all platforms; they spawn only `node scripts/build-extension.ts` and read JSON. G5 normalises `\` to `/` before the needle search on win32 and compares the sandbox root in both spellings (`windows-extension-builders` job already renders on `windows-latest`; golden overlay `.win32.golden` applies only to the Antigravity `hooks.json`, whose command carries `GUARDED_PREFIX`, so G7/G8 Antigravity on win32 must accept that prefix: *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME*).

- `ci/ci-capabilities.yml` capability `extension-render` (L1672) installs only `jq`/`yq`; the TS replacement needs Node, so the capability merges into `extension-builders-ts` (decision for the wiring owner, B-fragment merge).
- The 64-mask suite on `windows-latest` and its 300 s budget (see Platform).

#### Release engine suites and golden generator (pull request P3)

Shared facts for the three items below. `scripts/monorepo-release.sh` is already a forwarding shim over `scripts/monorepo-release.ts` (spec 0257 R28; entry 39 lines, modules in `scripts/lib/monorepo-release/*.ts`, notes facade in `scripts/lib/release-notes/`), so both suites run the shim over the TypeScript entry. Neither suite sources `scripts/lib/common.sh` nor any shell library: case 11g, the only in-process sourcing of `scripts/lib/monorepo-release-lib.sh`, was retired by spec 0257 PR 7 (engine suite header L33-34, L907-913; replaced by `rb-11g-release-note` in `scripts/tests/monorepo-release-blackbox-flows2.test.ts` L167). The R13 sourced-library exception therefore does NOT apply: no replacement is marked "Retires with scripts/lib/common.sh (row J4)". The script rows own the code under test: row J1 of spec 0257 (`scripts/monorepo-release.ts`, `release-package-extension.ts`, `release-pr.ts`), already implemented; the three items here are the row J2 oracles that spec 0257 R21 and its out-of-scope list (L548-550) explicitly left to J2.

**Open question for the coordinator (decision proposed, `VERIFY AT PLAN TIME`).** `test-monorepo-release-notes.sh` does NOT stay Bash for J4: 0257 R21 froze its assertions only until J2, and it asserts no shell-library behaviour. Proposal: both suites migrate in J2. The only reason to defer would be a sourced library, and there is none. Side finding: spec 0257 R28 says the `release-notes` job runs Node.js 22; in the current tree `.github/workflows/release-notes.yml` L40-45 already sets Node 24, so that remark is stale (VERIFY AT PLAN TIME against `.gitlab-ci.yml` L2957 image).

Overlap rule applied to both: the existing `scripts/tests/monorepo-release-*.test.ts` (21 files, 3716 lines) run the driver with a CAPTURING `npx` and a CAPTURING `release-rehearse.mjs` stub (`scripts/tests/lib/release-capture-stubs.ts`, `release-driver-sandbox.ts`) or unit-test one module; none runs the REAL semantic-release engine, the REAL gitmoji templates, a real bare origin plus the forge stub, or a real archive. That real-engine property is the whole value of the two Bash suites and is not covered anywhere else, so every case maps to a new file.

##### `scripts/tests/fixtures/release/generate-github-golden.sh` (77 lines)

- **What it is.** A golden generator, not a suite: it extracts the historical heredoc driver from git (`REF` default `bbd6cf6`), runs it unmodified in a throwaway fixture with a copying `npx` shim, normalises the fixture root to `/ROOT` with `jq` (`walk`, `split/join`), and prints `github-releaserc.golden.json`. Consumers of the golden: `monorepo-release-blackbox-flows2.test.ts`, `monorepo-release-process.test.ts`, `release-driver-sandbox.test.ts`, `lib/release-capture-stubs.ts`, `lib/windows-packaging-release.ts`, `test-monorepo-release.sh`. `fixtures/release/fixed-date.cjs` L1-15 names step 11g in its comment. No Bash suite asserts the generator itself.
- **Replacement.** `scripts/tests/fixtures/release/generate-github-golden.ts` (~90 lines, run with `node`; the `.sh` leaves `ci/shell-allowlist.txt` L110). Same algorithm: `git show <REF>:scripts/monorepo-release.sh`, same fixture (`foo` 1.2.0, branch `main`, one `fixture` commit), an `npx` shim copying `.releaserc.json`, literal-string replacement of the root (`split(root).join("/ROOT")` in a recursive walk, never a regex), key-sorted output equal to `jq -S`. It runs `bash` on the HISTORICAL script by necessity, so it is a POSIX-only developer tool. Open decision (`VERIFY AT PLAN TIME`): whether the TS generator is kept at all, or the golden is declared frozen and the generator deleted together with `github-releaserc.golden.json`'s last consumer (the Bash header says the heredoc exists only in history). New smoke test `scripts/tests/release-golden-generator.test.ts` (~110 lines): (a) the generator at the default ref reproduces the committed golden byte-for-byte (the oracle that the TS generator equals the shell one; the golden is frozen), (b) unknown ref: exit 2 and the same message, (c) a ref whose driver writes no config: exit 1 and the same message (candidate ref `VERIFY AT PLAN TIME`), (d) no host path in the output (`/ROOT` only; no `/private`, `/tmp`, `/Users`, `/home`).

| label | behaviour asserted | replacement::test intent | MUST survive |
|---|---|---|---|
| exit 2 | `REF` lacks `scripts/monorepo-release.sh`: `Error: <ref> has no scripts/monorepo-release.sh`, exit 2 | generator::unknown ref | message and status |
| exit 1 | no captured config: `Error: the driver at <ref> wrote no .releaserc.json`, exit 1 | generator::driver wrote nothing | message and status |
| golden bytes | output equals committed golden under `jq -S` | generator::reproduces golden | byte equality, not deep equality |
| root normalisation | every occurrence of the fixture root becomes `/ROOT`, literally | generator::no host path | literal replace incl. `release-output` paths |

- **Changed or dropped assertions.** Nothing is dropped; the comparison tightens from `jq -S` equality to byte equality (the committed file is already `jq -S` output).
- **Regeneration protocol (never on macOS).** (1) Run in a Linux container, Ubuntu 24.04, Node 24, GNU `tar` and `gzip`, repository mounted read-only, output to a scratch dir. (2) Regenerate in a SECOND image with Node under `/opt/hostedtoolcache/node/<v>/x64/bin`. (3) `diff -r` of the two outputs must be empty. (4) Host-specific values normalised: fixture root to `/ROOT` (already); no `PATH`, user name, temp dir or Node path in the golden (asserted by (d)). (5) The committed golden changes only when the historical ref changes, and never in the same pull request as a migration of `scripts/monorepo-release.ts` (parent R13). The ref must stay reachable (`git cat-file -e` is the first check), so a shallow CI clone cannot run it: the smoke test skips when the ref is absent and the `fetch-depth` decision is `VERIFY AT PLAN TIME`. The generator is not wired into CI.
- **Mutation set.** On a temp copy of the generator: (1) root normalised with a regex instead of a literal split -> (d) with a fixture root containing a dot or `+`. (2) drop the key sort -> golden bytes. (3) change the default ref -> golden bytes. (4) remove the `cat-file` guard -> exit 2. (5) stop exiting 1 on an empty capture -> exit 1.
- **Platform.** POSIX-only.
- **Static reads and wiring.** `ci/shell-allowlist.txt` L110; `specs/0257-packaging-release-typescript.md` L251 and L552 (assign it to J2); `scripts/tests/fixtures/release/fixed-date.cjs` L1-15; `scripts/tests/test-monorepo-release.sh` (consumer, names the generator in a usage comment). Not referenced by any workflow, `.gitlab-ci.yml`, `ci/ci-capabilities.yml`, `Taskfile.yml` or `docs/`.
- **Ratchet pitfalls.** `.sh`, `.js`, `.mjs` files count in the ratchet: the notes suite's runtime `driver.mjs` is written from a string inside the test; any stored script is suffixed `.golden` and guarded for a leading `#!`. Never run a real `gh` or publish; no release golden is generated against the real forge or on macOS.

#### Governance check suites (pull requests P4 and P5)

Common to the seven suites. Each check is now a forwarding shim `scripts/check-<name>.sh` of a TypeScript entry `scripts/check-<name>.ts` (row I1, spec 0259), and I1 already shipped a black-box edge-case test per check, `scripts/tests/governance-check-<name>.test.ts`. Those files say in their header that they cover only what the Bash suite does NOT; so every Bash case below needs a NEW test, except where the mapping names an existing one (each name verified by grep of `test(`/`it(` titles). New files are `scripts/tests/governance-<name>-cases.test.ts`, each under 300 lines. They share one helper `scripts/tests/lib/governance-sandbox.ts` (~90 lines: `mkdtemp` registry with `after` cleanup, `runEntry(entry, {cwd, env, args})` spawning `process.execPath --disable-warning=MODULE_TYPELESS_PACKAGE_JSON <entry>` with `LC_ALL=C`, `CI` deleted from the child env, `NODE_TEST_CONTEXT` deleted, and returning `{status, stdout, stderr}` with stderr compared; `copyTracked(dest, paths)` copying only `git ls-files` entries; `stageNodeModules(dest)` doing a REAL `fs.cpSync(..., {dereference: true})` of `node_modules/js-yaml` and `node_modules/argparse` plus `.git/` and the root `package.json`). Every sandbox mutates temp copies only; the checkout is never written. Platform for all seven: the entries are cross-platform Node, so the new files run on all platforms except where a case needs a symlink or a POSIX mode (marked). Windows coverage of the same entries already exists in `windows-governance-*.test.ts` and is untouched.

##### Shared notes for the seven suites

- Wiring removal pattern (same for all seven): delete the path filter line, the `ci-cache-guard --stray-scan -- bash <suite>` step, the three GitLab lines, the three `ci/ci-capabilities.yml` lines and the `ci/shell-allowlist.txt` line; `check-test-wiring` and `check-test-strays` then no longer see the `.sh`; confirm via their own tests (`governance-check-test-wiring.test.ts`, `governance-check-test-strays.test.ts`) that they expect no stale entry. VERIFY AT PLAN TIME that the allowlist shrink is mandatory (a ratchet) or only permitted.
- Ratchet pitfalls: no `.sh`/`.js` fixture in any new test; fixtures are written at run time or are `.golden`. Sandboxes copy git-tracked files plus a REAL copy of `js-yaml` and `argparse`; nested `node --test` runs unset `NODE_TEST_CONTEXT`; `CI` is deleted from the child env; `LC_ALL=C`; stderr is compared, not discarded.

Common facts for the seven suites below (stated once, applied to each):

- Every `scripts/check-<name>.sh` is a forwarding shim of `scripts/check-<name>.ts` (spec 0259 requirement 21; the shim runs `scripts/lib/node-floor-guard.js` then `exec node --disable-warning=MODULE_TYPELESS_PACKAGE_JSON scripts/check-<name>.ts "$@"`). The TypeScript logic lives in `scripts/lib/governance/*.ts`. A Bash oracle that mutates the shell therefore mutates the `.ts` twin: each mutant below names the `.ts` file (the `.sh` shim only has two mutable lines, the `exec` line and the floor-guard line; mutant S below covers them once for all seven suites).
- Existing `.test.ts` files from spec 0259 (I1) assert edge cases against FROZEN shell recordings under `scripts/tests/fixtures/governance-oracle/*.json.golden` (suffix `.golden` so the ratchet ignores them, verified by `ls`); they deliberately do NOT repeat the Bash suite's happy/sad cases (their headers say "edge cases the Bash suite does not cover"). So most Bash cases below need a NEW test; the exceptions are named per row.
- Replacement style (requirements 4-20 of this spec): one `node --test` file per suite, helper(s) under `scripts/tests/lib/`, each < 300 lines, the entry run as `process.execPath --disable-warning=MODULE_TYPELESS_PACKAGE_JSON scripts/check-<name>.ts` (the shim is exercised once by `governance-shims.test.ts`, VERIFY AT PLAN TIME that it asserts all seven names), `NODE_TEST_CONTEXT` unset and `CI` deleted from every child env, `LC_ALL=C`, stdout AND stderr compared where the Bash suite grepped them. Fixture trees are `mkdtemp` directories created by the test; sandboxes that need the real checkout copy only git-tracked files plus a REAL copy (not a symlink) of `node_modules/js-yaml` and `node_modules/argparse` (feedback-routing parses YAML; VERIFY AT PLAN TIME which of the seven entries import `js-yaml`).
- Shared helper (new): `scripts/tests/lib/governance-git-fixture.ts` (~80 lines): `initRepo(dir)` (`git init -q`, `user.email`, `user.name`, `commit.gpgsign false`, with `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL`/`GIT_CONFIG_SYSTEM` pointed at an empty file so the host config cannot leak), `commit(dir, msg)`, `runEntry(entry, cwd, args, env)` returning `{status, stdout, stderr}`. Used by the bump, skill-versions, no-machine-paths and markdown-links suites. Do not duplicate the `git()` helpers already inlined in `governance-check-extension-version-bump.test.ts` L25-45 and `governance-check-skill-versions.test.ts` L55: the new helper is for NEW files; migrating the old ones is out of scope (VERIFY AT PLAN TIME whether to do it for the 300-line ceiling).
- Mutant S (shim, applies to all seven): in a temp copy of `scripts/check-<name>.sh` change `exec node ... check-<name>.ts "$@"` to `exec node ... check-<name>.ts` (drop `"$@"`) -> goes red in `bump` (arg `HEAD~1`, cases 1-6) and `markdown-links` (file argument), proving the shim forwards arguments; for the five argument-less checks the shim is covered by `governance-shims.test.ts` only (VERIFY AT PLAN TIME). Mutate temp copies only, never the checkout.

None covers cases 1, 5, 6, 8 or Part B. The Part B byte-identity (R4) check is NOT covered anywhere (`grep -rn "byte" scripts/tests/*.test.ts` finds only `build-components-provenance.test.ts` L208, an unrelated `.md`-resource rule).

- **Changed or dropped assertions.** Bash read exit codes only (stdout/stderr to `/dev/null`); the replacement ALSO asserts the verdict line on pass and the offending path on fail (strengthening; the wording is frozen so this does not couple to new text). The B3 `jq` skip becomes `{ skip: ... }` with the printed reason; under `CI` it must not skip silently: `ci/ci-capabilities.yml` job `extension-provenance` (L1829-1850) installs `jq` (VERIFY AT PLAN TIME), and if it does, add a sentinel assertion that `jq` is present when `CI` is set. B1 and B2 comment lines naming `install-extension.sh` L50/L53 and `build-claude-plugin.sh` L128 are stale line numbers; replace by function names (spec 0255 delta-01 L141 already documents that this suite only mentions those scripts). Nothing else dropped.
- **Mutation set.** (1) `scripts/lib/governance/ext-provenance.ts`: drop the feedback==canonical comparison -> Case 3. (2) same file: stop requiring `feedback` in the field list -> Case 4. (3) same file: stop skipping `extensions/org` (or add it to the walked tiers) -> Case 6. (4) same file: accept the carrier on any line instead of the first body line (use `carrier.ts` `firstBodyLine` -> a global search) -> Case 8. (5) same file: skip `agents/*/AGENT.md` -> Case 5 (an agent without carrier must be paired with a complete one; the pair proves the walk, add a carrier-less agent to Case 5's twin if needed, VERIFY AT PLAN TIME). (6) same file: return success when no carrier found -> Case 2. (7) `scripts/lib/governance/carrier.ts` `firstBodyLine`: stop skipping blank lines -> Case 1 stays green only if the carrier is directly after the fence (it is), so use the greeter in R4 setup. (8) the greeter fixture: append a trailing space to the real carrier line in a temp copy of the source tree used by B3 -> B1/B2/B3 stay green (byte identity holds); the mutant that must go red for B3 is `scripts/build-claude-plugin.ts` rewriting the skill body (e.g. trim a trailing newline in the skill copy) -> B3 only. ##### Wiring common to the seven suites

- `ci/shell-allowlist.txt` L129-132, L134, L136, L138 lose their entries in the same pull request that deletes each suite (the allowlist shrink check, VERIFY AT PLAN TIME which script enforces it).
- The `check-test-wiring` expectation: every new `*.test.ts` must be named by a CI job. The seven CI jobs above keep a `--stray-scan -- bash <suite>` command today; each is rewritten to `node --disable-warning=MODULE_TYPELESS_PACKAGE_JSON --test <new files>` in all three CI files (GitHub, GitLab, `ci/ci-capabilities.yml`, which is the reference the wiring tests read), and the existing governance `.test.ts` files named in those jobs (if any) stay. `check-test-strays` fails on a `.test.ts` not wired: new files must be wired in the same PR.
- `scripts/tests/build-components-fixture-closure.test.ts` `LITERALS`: none of the seven Bash suites contains an inline `js-yaml argparse` literal (`grep -l "js-yaml\|argparse"` on the seven files returns nothing), so no entry is added or removed; the new TS sandboxes that need `js-yaml` for `feedback-routing` copy it through the existing closure helper (`scripts/tests/lib/build-fixture-tree.ts`, VERIFY AT PLAN TIME) rather than a literal.
- Ratchet (`scripts/check-ratchet.ts`): any `.sh` or `.js` fixture added under `scripts/tests/` counts toward the ratchet; this fragment adds none (all fixtures are generated at runtime into `mkdtemp` directories); golden files, if any, carry the `.golden` suffix.
- Count sentinel: each table-driven file asserts its own case count (`assert.equal(cases.length, N)`; N = 8 provenance, 5+3 bump, 6 feedback-routing, 8 no-machine-paths groups, 3 markdown-links, 4+3 skill-versions) so that a deleted row cannot pass silently.

#### Governance guards and spec linter (pull request P6)

Common to the three suites below. The three checks each ship as a Bash forwarding shim of a TypeScript entry
(row I1, spec 0259): `scripts/check-gemini-overlay-enrollment.sh` -> `.ts` -> `scripts/lib/governance/gemini-overlay.ts`
(160 lines), `scripts/check-test-strays.sh` -> `.ts` -> `scripts/lib/governance/test-strays.ts` (100 lines),
`scripts/check-test-wiring.sh` -> `.ts` -> `scripts/lib/governance/test-wiring.ts` (156 lines). Behaviour is frozen.
A replacement test runs the `.ts` entry directly (`process.execPath --disable-warning=MODULE_TYPELESS_PACKAGE_JSON`),
as the existing governance tests do; the shim itself keeps its coverage in `scripts/tests/governance-shims.test.ts`
(VERIFY AT PLAN TIME that it names these three shims). Every spawn deletes `CI`, `GITHUB_*`, `CI_*` from the child
environment, sets `LC_ALL=C`, and compares stdout and stderr byte for byte (the Bash suites grep substrings; the
replacement compares the full frozen text, which is stricter). Fixtures are written at run time under `mkdtemp`
(never tracked), so the `.sh`/`.js` ratchet and `check-test-wiring` see nothing new. No test spawns a nested
`node --test`; if one is needed, `NODE_TEST_CONTEXT` is deleted from its env. The replacement files of all three
suites share one helper, `scripts/tests/lib/governance-check-run.ts` (~60 lines: `runEntry(entry, repoDir, {args, env})`
returning `{status, stdout, stderr}`, with the env scrubbing above, and `mkRepo(prefix)` registering the directory for
removal in an `after` hook).

##### `scripts/tests/test-spec-linter.sh` (1497 lines)

- **Script under test and ownership.** `scripts/lib/spec-linter.js` (792 lines, CommonJS, requires `js-yaml` and `semver`, spawns `npx --no-install markdownlint` and `git`). No migration row owns it: grep of `specs/0215*`, `0250`, `0259`, `0262` finds it only in 0215 R16 (L242-246, "the JavaScript already tracked when the ratchet lands ... MAY be typed in place when a change touches it, SHALL NOT grow") and in the 0215 out-of-scope list (L483-487, "a mandatory conversion of the existing JavaScript ... `scripts/lib/spec-linter.js` and similar"). It is entry L8 of `ci/js-baseline.txt` (shrink-only ratchet) and is invoked by `Taskfile.yml` L577 (`spec:lint`). So it is a retained JavaScript file with no node-floor exception row and it stays JavaScript. Consequence: the suite CAN move to TypeScript now, with no dependency on any other row; the TypeScript suite drives the unchanged `.js` through a child `node` process (behaviour frozen, the file is not edited, no baseline entry changes, no version bump). Typing it in place is NOT part of this row.
- **Replacement suite.** Groups in file order: S1 = Cases 1-16 and 41-43 (single-file rules, no git), S2 = Cases 17-27 incl. 26b (specs/org and manifest exclusion, cross-file id uniqueness), S3 = Cases 28-34 (base-branch `draft` check: identification, base-ref derivation, skip, uncanonicalizable path), S4 = Cases 35-40 (attribution, working-directory independence), S5 = Cases 44-48 and 52-59 (status transitions and implementation-branch check), S6 = Cases 49-51 (dependency preflights). Overlap check done (`ls scripts/tests`, grep `spec-linter|spec:lint` over `scripts/tests/*.test.ts`): no existing `.test.ts` runs the linter; `derive-spec-status.test.ts` and `model-ci-tooling-merge-spec-pr.test.ts` match only on "spec" and cover other scripts. `scripts/tests/test-check-spec-id-reserved.sh` and `test-reserve-spec-id.sh` only mention the linter in comments (other fragments). - **Changed or dropped assertions.** (1) `run_case`'s `$files` word splitting and the `Pass`/`FAIL` counter and `Results:` summary are dropped; `node --test` reports. (2) Every "output" grep is `grep -F` on stdout+stderr merged (`2>&1`); the replacement asserts on the stream that carries the text (the linter writes findings to stderr via `console.error`), keeping a merged-stream variant only where the Bash did, and compares stderr exactly where a case is a pure content case. (3) Case 33, which relied on `$TMP_ROOT` having no git ancestor, now builds its root under `fs.realpathSync(os.tmpdir())` and asserts no `.git` in any ancestor (the Bash did not; stronger). (4) Case 50: PATH becomes `dirname(process.execPath)` + `/usr/bin` + `/bin` (the Bash used `command -v node`); `NPM_CONFIG_PREFIX` kept. (5) Cases 28-59 that did not pin `BRANCH_NAME` (28-47) inherited ambient `GITHUB_HEAD_REF`, `CI_MERGE_REQUEST_SOURCE_BRANCH_NAME`, `GITHUB_REF_NAME`, `CI_COMMIT_REF_NAME` only when the fixture has no resolvable git branch (L285-306 order: `BRANCH_NAME`, local git, CI variables); the child env now deletes all five, plus `CI`, `BASE_REF`, `BRANCH_NAME` by default and sets them per case (a strengthening: removes an ambient-CI leak the Bash had in Case 33 and Case 29). (6) Nothing is dropped: all 59 numbered cases, the `26b`, `32b`, `32c`, `52a-c`, `53`-`58` sub-runs and `56a-b`, `57a-b`, each map above.
- **Isolation (pitfalls).**
  - Sandbox: a `mkdtemp` directory holding a COPY of `scripts/lib/spec-linter.js` at `<sb>/scripts/lib/spec-linter.js` plus a copy of `.crewrig/core-paths.txt` at `<sb>/.crewrig/` (so the default `repoDir`, `__dirname/../..`, L72, resolves in the sandbox and mutation runs mutate the copy; only git-tracked files are copied, enumerated with `git ls-files`), `.markdownlintrc` in each case root, and a REAL copy (not a symlink) of `node_modules/js-yaml`, `node_modules/argparse` (js-yaml's dependency) and `node_modules/semver` into `<sb>/node_modules`. Real copies matter because Node resolves a symlinked package from its realpath and would climb back into the checkout's `node_modules`, defeating Case 49. `markdownlint-cli` is resolved by `npx --no-install` from the case root: copy `node_modules/.bin/markdownlint` and the `markdownlint-cli` tree only if copying works, otherwise symlink the whole `node_modules` exactly as the Bash did for Cases 1-48 (*VERIFY AT PLAN TIME*; a symlinked `node_modules` is read-only here and the cleanup uses `fs.rmSync(root, {recursive: true})`, which unlinks a symlink without following it, but cleanup must never run on a path not created by `mkdtemp`).
  - Cases 49 and 51 use a root with no `node_modules` in any ancestor (disjoint `mkdtemp`, not a child of the sandbox); Case 50 builds its own `node_modules` with real copies of js-yaml/argparse/semver only.
  - Child env: `delete NODE_TEST_CONTEXT` (otherwise a nested `node` inherits the runner context and its output is swallowed), delete `CI`, `BASE_REF`, `BRANCH_NAME`, `GITHUB_HEAD_REF`, `GITHUB_REF_NAME`, `CI_MERGE_REQUEST_SOURCE_BRANCH_NAME`, `CI_COMMIT_REF_NAME`, `CREWRIG_REPO_DIR`; set `LC_ALL=C`; `HOME` to a scratch dir; `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null`, `GIT_CONFIG_SYSTEM=/dev/null` (so `diff.relative`, `init.defaultBranch`, signing never leak, Case 40 sets `diff.relative` locally).
  - Ratchet: no `.js` or `.sh` fixture file is committed (the broken `js-yaml/index.js` of Case 51 and every spec fixture are generated at run time by `fs.writeFileSync` into the `mkdtemp` root); if a static fixture proves unavoidable it takes the `.golden` suffix.
  - Branch-prefix rule: the linter reads `^(?:feat|fix|refactor|perf|chore)/(\d{4})-` (L752) as an implementation branch and, apart from `chore/<NNNN>-sync-main*` (L753), demands `status: implemented` on every file whose `related-issue` equals the resolved ticket. The repo's own pull-request branch for this row therefore must not use those prefixes until the last pull request: use `test/<ticket>-<slug>` (the `test/` prefix is outside the regex) until the final PR; `feat/` would make `spec:lint` demand `status: implemented` on spec 0265 while it is not yet. The tests never rely on the real branch: every implementation case pins `BRANCH_NAME` or creates its own fixture branch.
  - Timing: every case runs `node` plus `npx markdownlint` plus `git` (about 0.5-1.5 s each on Linux, more on macOS); about 70 child runs, so S1 and S2 batch where the Bash did and `node --test` file-level parallelism covers the rest; a per-test timeout of 60 s (*VERIFY AT PLAN TIME*, measure before fixing the budget).
- **Mutation set.** All on the sandbox copy of `scripts/lib/spec-linter.js` (never the checkout). (1) L752 regex drop `refactor|perf|chore` is NOT covered by the suite (no case uses those prefixes except 57a); record as an accepted gap or add a `refactor/` case (*VERIFY AT PLAN TIME*). (2) L753 remove `&& !/^chore\/\d{4}-sync-main/...test(currentBranch)` -> 57a. (3) L753 widen the guard to any prefix -> 57b. (4) L413/L546 restore `!isDelta` on base-branch identification (around L695-706) -> 28 (delta named), 52a-c. (5) restore `!isDelta` on the 0168 R1 filter (around L729) -> 53 run 1. (6) restrict implementation matching to `id === nnnn` (L755) -> 54, 55, 56b. (7) remove the `[NOTICE]` emit (L769) -> 48, 59. (8) delete `gitCwd = repoRoot` (L360) -> 39, 40. (9) `process.exit(2)` to `1` at L366 -> 32 (exact 2). (10) drop the `endsWith('/')` normalisation (L182-184) -> 32b. (11) drop the `develop` fallback (L196-199) -> 32c. (12) delete the empty-change-set branch so it warns -> 35. (13) invert the `changed.has` test so a touched offender warns -> 37, 52c. (14) turn the "could not be derived" branch (L689-690) into a warning -> 38. (15) `requireLintDependency`: `if (err.code !== 'MODULE_NOT_FOUND') throw err` removed (L13-17) -> 51. (16) L601 preflight message text change -> 50. (17) exclusion applied only in the per-file loop, not before `fileResults` (L621-622) -> 26. (18) default id to the filename prefix on parse failure (L433-441) -> 27. (19) markdownlint `-c` path read from the script directory rather than cwd -> 8 (and 39/40, where config sits under `sub/`). (20) hardcode `specs/org` in place of the manifest (L100-115) -> 20, 26. Line numbers are those of the base commit and may shift; confirm at plan time.
- **Platform.** POSIX-only (`skip` on win32: symlinks, `npx` shim, `git` fixture repositories and `symbolic-ref`, `PATH` of `/usr/bin:/bin`). All six test files run on Linux and macOS; the Linux job (`ci/ci-capabilities.yml`) is the authoritative one, macOS is run locally. `core-paths` tab separators in manifests are written with literal `\t`.

#### Harness curator (pull request P7)

##### `artifacts/library/skills/harness-curator/scripts/test.sh` (1335 lines)

- **What it does.** One linear script, no functions per case beyond helpers. It drives the three skill entries through their `.sh` shims (which `exec node <name>.ts`, Node 24 floor) and, for `apply.py` and `curate.py`, directly through `python3` or `importlib`. Groups in file order: G1 = L50-130 (fixture run `curate.sh --from-stdin --dry-run` on `assets/sample-frictions.json`, 16 drawers, stats block); G2 = L132-285 (spec 0010 `skipped[]` / `routing_failures[]`, yq-merge and gh-body-truncation cluster shape, spec 0228 title prefixes, issue #69 resolved filter); G3 = L287-418 (spec 0032/0033 block scalars drw-011..016, resolved precedence); G4 = L420-471 (spec 0228 `curate.py` unit calls through `curate_eval`); G5 = L473-558 (`apply.py --dry-run-apply`); G6 = L560-577 (`dedup_match` wire shape); G7 = L579-633 (`--max-issues`); G8 = L635-659 (`schedule-curator.sh --uninstall`); G9 = L661-725 (issue #63 `norm.*`); G10 = L727-904 (spec 0105 forge-agnostic argv, dedup argv, `_match_existing`, delta-02 Gitmoji matching); G11 = L906-958 (issue #1272 `_stamp_drawer`, `global` line guard); G12 = L960-1018 (`setup-labels.sh` dry-run plan and usage errors); G13 = L1020-1204 (real MemPalace path, gated on `mempalace` CLI + importable `mempalace.mcp_server`); G14 = L1206-1335 (spec 0060 malformed-rate warning W1-W3).

No `.test.ts` anywhere asserts `apply.py` (no `apply*.test.*` exists), `curate.py` units, the fixture stats, or the 0060 warning.
