- **Changed or dropped assertions.** (1) The `FAIL: jq is required` and `python3 is required` prerequisite probes become `findTool("jq")` is no longer needed: `JSON.parse` replaces every `jq` filter, so jq leaves the capability list (VERIFY AT PLAN TIME whether `ci/ci-capabilities.yml` `tools: [jq]` drops; the `check-skill-versions` job shares it). `python3` stays a hard requirement, asserted by a `before` hook that fails (not skips) under `CI` and skips with a printed reason locally when absent. (2) The Bash `chmod +x` side effects on `curate.sh` and `setup-labels.sh` are dropped (a test must not write the tracked tree); the executable bit is asserted instead on POSIX. (3) `assert` text comparison of `jq -r` output (strings) becomes typed `deepEqual`; no weakening, `null` and `false` are asserted as such. (4) G8: the Bash test runs `--uninstall` against the REAL home and the real scheduler (launchd/systemd/Task Scheduler) of the machine; the replacement runs it under a scratch HOME with scratch `XDG_*`, `LOCALAPPDATA` unset and stub `launchctl`, `systemctl`, `crontab`, `schtasks` that record calls into a sentinel file, and asserts the managed plist/timer path in the message lies under the scratch HOME. This is a behavioural improvement forced by the host-safety rule, not a loss. (5) G13: the Bash test resolves `mempalace` through `command -v` and the real pipx venv and sets only `MEMPALACE_PALACE_PATH`; the replacement also scratches HOME (see below) and no longer SKIPs silently: it keeps the SKIP when the pipx venv is absent, with the printed reason, and never fails under `CI` for a missing mempalace (the `test-harness-curate` job installs none, so it skips there as it does today). (6) The shared EXIT `trap` chain (three `trap` re-assignments overwrite one another in Bash, so MAX_FIXTURE and the real tmpdir were in fact leaked after the later trap) becomes `after` hooks per file removing only mkdtemp paths. (7) The `set +e` capture of `$?` becomes `spawnSync` status; a signal-killed child (null status) fails the test, never reads as 0. (8) The G13 `grep`/shell `SEEDED_DRAWER_ID` dup-fd technique is KEPT inside the Python seeding snippet (`os.dup(1)` before importing `mempalace.mcp_server`, `closefd=False`), as the repository's MemPalace rule requires. Nothing else dropped; every case of G1-G14 appears once above.

- **Isolation (scratch palace, stub `gh`, no real state).** `scratchEnv()` builds, per test file, `root = mkdtemp(<os.tmpdir()>/crewrig-hc-)` with `root/home`, `root/palace`, `root/bin`, `root/state`, and returns an env object built from an allow-list, not from `process.env`: `PATH` = `root/bin` + the directory of `node` + the directories holding `bash`, `python3` (or the resolved `MEMPALACE_PYTHON`) and nothing else that holds a `gh`, `glab`, `tea`, `launchctl`, `systemctl`, `crontab` or `schtasks`; `HOME` and `USERPROFILE` = `root/home`; `XDG_CONFIG_HOME`, `XDG_STATE_HOME`, `XDG_DATA_HOME` under `root/home`; `MEMPALACE_PALACE_PATH` = `root/palace`; `CREWRIG_*` forge variables unset; `NODE_TEST_CONTEXT` and `CI` deleted; `LC_ALL=C`; `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1`; `PYTHONNOUSERSITE=1`. Stub `gh` (a `bash` script written at test time into `root/bin`, mode 0755, never committed so the ratchet counts nothing) appends its argv, one JSON array per line, to `root/state/gh-calls.jsonl`, answers `auth status` with exit 0 and `issue list` with `[]`, and exits 1 on `issue create` (the suite never applies; `--dry-run-apply` must not reach it, asserted by the file staying empty). The stub scheduler tools do the same into `root/state/sched-calls.jsonl`. `assertNoRealState()` runs in every file's `after`: (a) the call logs contain no `issue create`, no `launchctl load`/`bootstrap`, no `systemctl enable`, no `crontab -` write, no `schtasks /create`; (b) `root/home` was the only HOME in any child env (the helper refuses an env whose `HOME` is not under `os.tmpdir()`); (c) before and after the file, the mtime and size of the real `~/.mempalace` (if present) and the real `~/Library/LaunchAgents/*crewrig*` are unchanged (read-only stat, `VERIFY AT PLAN TIME`: on a clean machine both are absent and stay absent); (d) the palace path used by G13 is asserted to be inside `os.tmpdir()` and not equal to `$HOME/.mempalace`, before any seeding, with a hard failure (never skip) if not. Palace: G13 seeds through the mempalace interpreter (`MEMPALACE_PYTHON`, else the pipx venv from `PIPX_HOME`, as `curate.ts` L203-219 resolves it; the env passes `PIPX_HOME` read-only for that lookup only) running `tool_add_drawer` under `MEMPALACE_PALACE_PATH=root/palace`. Because a stdio-lock peer (the real daemon) would refuse writes on the real palace, the test also asserts the daemon is irrelevant: it never opens a socket. No `mempalace` shell command is run; the CLI probe `command -v mempalace` becomes `import mempalace.mcp_server` through the interpreter only (the CLI binary is not required by the library path; VERIFY AT PLAN TIME that dropping the CLI probe does not change when the group skips on the CI image). Pitfalls observed: sandboxes copy only git-tracked files, so tests read `assets/sample-frictions.json` and the `.py` sources from the checkout (or `CREWRIG_REPO_UNDER_TEST`, default the checkout) and write nothing there; nested `node --test` is not used (the shims `exec node <name>.ts`, a plain child), but `NODE_TEST_CONTEXT` is still deleted so a future nested run behaves.

- **Mutation set.** Mutate temp copies of git-tracked files only (copy `artifacts/library/skills/harness-curator/` into mkdtemp and point `CREWRIG_REPO_UNDER_TEST` at it). Each mutant must turn the named case red and nothing else must be needed. `curate.ts`: (1) drop the `--max-issues` truncation `slice` (or set `clusters_truncated` always 0) -> G7 count and `clusters_truncated`. (2) swap the severity ranking comparator -> G7 order. (3) emit `clusters_truncated` only when truncating -> G1 `clusters_truncated` key. (4) print the malformed-rate warning on stdout -> G14 W1 clean-stdout. (5) change the 25 percent threshold to 15 -> G14 W2. (6) warn when `total_drawers` is 0 -> G14 W3. `curate.py`: (7) change the `_dominant_room` fallback emoji `🔧` -> `🐛` -> G4 unmapped and no-`_room`. (8) compute the label tally independently of `_dominant_room` -> G4 shared. (9) restore the empty-suggestion check before the `opened_as` check -> G3 resolved (drw-013) and G1 `skipped_resolved`. (10) truncate block-scalar capture to its first line -> G3 multiline and indicators. (11) let the block capture swallow the next key -> G3 generalized. (12) change `BLOCK_SCALAR_RE` to drop `\|-` -> G3 strip. (13) drop the `severity:high` bypass -> G1 `clusters_above_threshold` and G2 gh-body-truncation. (14) drop the `(single day)` branch -> G2. (15) return `missing_canonical` as `no_canonical` -> G2 routing. (16) stamp not honoured: skip filter on `opened_as` removed -> G2 exclusions and G13 second run. (17) write stdout to the swapped fd (remove the dup of fd 1) -> G13 stdout non-empty. `apply.py`: (18) `--repo` keeps the `/blob/` suffix -> G9 and G5 slug. (19) remove the `stripping to repo root` warning -> G9 blob and tree. (20) gitea `--fields` loses `,url` -> G10 dedup. (21) `_match_existing` returns `item.get("url")` -> G10 match truthiness. (22) drop the trailing  `(` anchor -> G10 `k-merge` collision. (23) strip two leading tokens -> G10 one-token strip. (24) `existing_issue_url` raises on `FileNotFoundError` -> G10 fail-open. (25) `_stamp_drawer` ignores the second read -> G11 lying success. (26) `_stamp_drawer` calls `update_drawer` after a `get_drawer` error -> G11 B. (27) delete the `global tool_get_drawer, tool_update_drawer` line -> G11 global. (28) `glab` argv uses `--body` -> G10 gitlab. (29) `CREWRIG_GITLAB_HOSTS` ignored -> G10 R9. (30) emit `dedup_match` only when matched -> G6 count. `setup-labels.ts`: (31) drop one label from the table -> G12 plan 9. (32) drop the `room:` family -> G12 family room. (33) color to five hex digits -> G12 shape. (34) accept `--bogus` -> G12 usage. `schedule-curator.ts`: (35) `--uninstall` exits 1 when nothing is installed -> G8. #### Model and CI tooling suites (pull requests P8 and P9; gated by requirement 22)

Oracle rule: these three suites are the Bash oracles of the row-I2 scripts. They migrate only AFTER the switch pull request of #1339, when `scripts/check-model-mappings.sh`, `scripts/check-agent-profiles.sh` and `scripts/build-ci.sh` are forwarding shims over `scripts/check-model-mappings.ts`, `scripts/check-agent-profiles.ts` and `scripts/build-ci.ts` (spec 0262 requirements 1, 16, 26). Until then the unchanged Bash suites run through the differential wrapper (`scripts/tests/lib/bash-suite-differential.ts` `installWrapper`, proven by `scripts/tests/model-ci-tooling-differential.test.ts`, 146 lines). That file holds only the wrapper's positive controls and the scratch-remote guards, NOT the per-script comparisons, so no assertion of the three suites below is "already covered" by a differential test: the wrapper compares the shell and the twin on every fixture the Bash suite builds, which is a byte-level superset of the suites' exit-plus-substring assertions, but it disappears with the Bash suites. The replacement suites therefore assert the TypeScript entries directly (shell leg gone: oracle rule). Names of the yq-accessor shared module and of the per-pass modules under `scripts/lib/` are fixed by the I2 PLAN: *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME* (they matter only for the mutation set). The shell-side mutants are dropped once the shims are the only shell code left; the shim itself is mutated only for the case that the shim contract test of spec 0259 does not already cover.

Common helpers (new, shared by the three suites, each file under 300 lines): `scripts/tests/lib/model-ci-sandbox.ts` (~120 lines: `mkdtemp` root; copies ONLY git-tracked files (`git ls-files -z`) plus a REAL copy (never a symlink) of `node_modules/js-yaml` and `node_modules/argparse`; `.git` and the root `package.json`; `runEntry(root, entry, args, env)` spawns `process.execPath --disable-warning=MODULE_TYPELESS_PACKAGE_JSON scripts/<entry>.ts` with `LC_ALL=C`, `CI` and `NODE_TEST_CONTEXT` deleted from the child env, returns `{status, stdout, stderr}`, stderr kept for comparison) and `scripts/tests/lib/model-mapping-fixtures.ts` (~150 lines: the `render_base`, `render_zero_offerings_valid`, `render_org_silent` documents as TypeScript objects dumped with `js-yaml`, plus `mutate(doc, fn)` replacing the `yq eval -i` expressions by plain object edits on a `structuredClone`). Fixtures stored as files (none are required: all are generated) would carry the `.golden` suffix and no leading `#!` (ratchet).

Hermetic root: `setup_pass3_root` (copies `scripts/lib/model-resolve.sh` into each root for the shell to source) is setup, not an assertion (spec 0262 requirement 17): after the pre-switch retarget the root holds only an empty `model-mappings/`, and the twin imports its modules from the checkout; `CREWRIG_REPO_DIR` points at the root. In the migrated suite the root is a bare `mkdtemp` directory with `model-mappings/`, nothing copied from `scripts/lib`.

- **Changed or dropped assertions.** (1) Exit-2 case: the twin needs no `yq` (spec 0262 requirements 4, 5); replace with `rejections::runs with PATH stripped of yq` asserting baseline exit 0; the shell-only exit 2 is dropped (shim behaviour without Node is the shim contract's). *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME* the twin has no `yq` probe. (2) `yq` fixture editing and `yq -r` reads become `js-yaml` loads and object mutations (`mutate`); fixtures are dumped from objects, so quoting differs, the checker reads values. (3) Delta-03: A0 asserts exit 1 and `: A0`  only, never the parser text (the entry prints the `js-yaml` message; the differential masks it). (4) Delta-02 24(c)/(d)/(e) behaviours (an unreadable input, multi-document YAML rejected with `Error:`, an id starting with `-` accepted literally) are not in the Bash suite; ADD three direct tests of those deliberate deviations (requirement 24(d), (e) are asserted "directly" by their spec), flagged as new rather than migrated. (5) S2 `assert_selection_table` prints `MISMATCH rung=...` lines; the replacement uses `assert.deepEqual` of the seven-element array, so the diagnostics come from node. (6) S1b `labels` uses `awk | sort -u`; replaced by `Set` plus sort in byte order. (7) Combined `2>&1` kept as union for id checks; stderr of green cases asserted empty (strengthening). Nothing else dropped.
- **Mutation set.** Temp copies only; the hermetic root removes the need to touch `scripts/lib/model-resolve.sh`. Mutants of `scripts/check-model-mappings.ts` and its pass modules (names *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME*), plus `scripts/lib/model-resolve/**` where selection lives: (1) skip duplicate-id check -> A6. (2) skip duplicate-rank check -> A7 and scenario 7. (3) skip the mapping-in-force pass -> scenario 7, scenario 9. (4) label pass 3 with the core file name instead of `(mapping in force)` -> scenarios 7 and 9 (negative greps). (5) drop `A26` co-firing in the `A11` branch -> A11. (6) let `A24` fire when declares strings differ -> A25 regression (the absence leg). (7) reopen the aspect vocabulary (accept `provides.intelligence.rung`) -> A25 regression. (8) selection tie-break to the lowest rank -> golden tables and both falsifiability cases. (9) `--print-selection` label from the materialised path -> label-set case. (10) org-only key `remove` accepted in a core file -> core A3. (11) `remove: [guard]` accepted -> A29 (both). (12) parse failure exits 0 -> A0. (13) zero offerings reported as an error -> zero-offerings case. (14) edit committed `model-mappings/claude.yml` in a TEMP COPY of the checkout (never the checkout): delete haiku, change a native value -> golden claude and the content rows. Each tied to the case(s) named; shell-side counterparts (the same ids in `check-model-mappings.sh`) are dropped with the oracle.
- **Platform.** All platforms for S1, S1b, S2, S3 (PowerShell runs the entry; printed paths use forward slashes by deviation 24(b), so the `(mapping in force)` label text, which is `model-mappings/<target>`, is path-free and stable). The committed-content tests read the checkout directly. Windows ignores `LC_ALL=C`; ordering is by byte order (requirement 3).
- **Static reads and wiring.** `.github/workflows/build.yml` L1800 (path filter), L1812 (`ci-cache-guard --stray-scan -- bash scripts/tests/test-check-model-mappings.sh`); `.gitlab-ci.yml` L2291, L2299, L2306; `ci/ci-capabilities.yml` L2827, L2835, L2842 (capability `check-model-mappings`); `ci/shell-allowlist.txt` L135 (remove); `docs/model-mapping-format.md` L429 (names the suite; update to the new file names, no version bump needed: docs only); `model-mappings/antigravity.yml` L152 (a comment naming `scripts/tests/test-check-model-mappings.sh` as the home of the golden per-rung table: changing it touches a committed mapping, so the pull request MUST keep the checks green and the golden data identical; the comment is a text-only edit, *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME* that no hash or fixture pins the mapping's bytes); `scripts/tests/test-model-resolution.sh` L5 (comment "idiom"); `specs/0262-model-ci-tooling-typescript.md` L195, L341 (name the suite and its fixture); delta-03 L14 (names the suite); `specs/0170-efficient-test-strays-guard.delta-01.md` L27 (historical). Spec 0262 requirement 17 retargets `setup_pass3_root` BEFORE the switch (assertions unchanged); by the time this row runs that retarget is in, so the migrated suite carries no `model-resolve.sh` copy. Sourced-library use: none in the suite itself; the Bash suite's only link to `scripts/lib/model-resolve.sh` was the `setup_pass3_root` copy (setup, retired). `test-model-resolution.sh` and `test-component-tier-resolution.sh` source the library in process and are NOT mine: per R13 exception they port as bash-spawning POSIX-only tests marked 'Retires with scripts/lib/common.sh (row J4)' only if they do source `common.sh` (they source `model-resolve.sh`: the marker text for them is *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME*, owned by the fragment that covers them). `build-components-fixture-closure.test.ts` LITERALS: register `model-ci-sandbox.ts` (see above).

##### `scripts/tests/test-model-resolution.sh` (1433 lines)

This section describes the migration to be done AFTER the I2 switch pull request (spec 0262 and deltas 01-04; the
switch is not merged at the time of writing): the Bash suite stays the oracle until then (parent R13 oracle rule),
and `scripts/lib/model-resolve.sh` retires WITH this suite in the I2 library-retirement pull request (spec 0262
requirement 17), not at row J4. Row J2 therefore owns the migration of the black-box groups; the sourced-library
groups are mapped to substitutes that 0262 already ships.

- **What the suite does.** Sections in file order: S1 = L55-175 (the real-tree check run once, `C2(a)`, `C2(b)` x3,
  `C2(c)`, `O0`), S2 = L176-562 (rule engine through `resolve_agent` sourced from `model-resolve.sh`: C1, C3-C7,
  C13(i), rules (b)/(d)/(e), R24/D16, (g)(5), (g)(6), D12/D17, C10, C9, M7), S3 = L564-710 (real builds of a probe
  agent in throwaway roots: C11, C12, C13(iii)/(ii), R21), S4 = L712-797 (M1-M6, R30), S5 = L799-1421 (organisation
  channel O1-O12, M8, M11), S6 = L1423-1433 (`O0 (closing)`, the summary). It sources `scripts/lib/model-resolve.sh` in
  process (via `build-components.sh`'s `--resolve` neighbours and the explicit `. "$M7_LIB"` at L549) for every
  `resolve_agent` call; it spawns `bash scripts/build-components.sh` and `scripts/tests/test-check-model-mappings`'s
  checker (`check-model-mappings.sh --print-selection`) as black boxes; it needs `yq` (the `yq` oracle pin of delta-04
  is `v4.54.1`, see Platform).
- **Overlap check.** Done by grep over `scripts/tests/*.test.ts` and `scripts/tests/lib/`. Already ported by spec 0250
  (R19-R21) and 0262 and green on `main`: `model-resolve-select.test.ts` (199 lines: C1, C3, C4, C13(i), rule (b), R7
  tail), `model-resolve-degraded.test.ts` (244: C5, C6, rule (b)), `model-resolve-cases.test.ts` (265: C7 incl. the
  checker A9 rejection, C10, D12/D17, M1, M2, M3, M4 incl. the `--print-selection` table, M6, rules (d)/(e), R24/D16,
  (g)(5), (g)(6)), `model-resolve-guidance.test.ts` (157: M5), `model-resolve-merge-nodes.test.ts` (251: O1, O5, O6,
  O6b), `model-resolve-merge-ops.test.ts` (234: O2, O3, O4, O9, O12), `model-resolve-merge-root.test.ts` (293: O7, O10
  live-handle, O11 + M11, M10), `build-components-cleanup.test.ts` (245: O10 main path and `--resolve` arm, retired
  M10), `build-components-conformance*.test.ts` (shell/TypeScript twin conformance, Linux, retire with the library
  per 0262 requirement 17), `lib/mapping-fixtures.ts` (builders named by this suite's `# --- <label>` headings),
  `model-resolve-mapping-access.test.ts` (276: accessors on the real mappings). NOT covered by any Node test, hence the
  new files: the real-tree gate (`C2(a)`, `C2(b)`, `C2(c)`, `O0`, closing `O0`), the `C9` drift and the seven-site CI
  count, `R39`, and the built-output cases `C11`, `C12`, `C13(ii)`/`C13(iii)`, `R21`, `R30`, plus a substitute for the
  two mutation cases that rewrite the shell library (`M7`, `M8`/`O8`). `scripts/tests/test-agent-profile-migration.sh`
  and `test-check-model-mappings.sh` are other rows' suites (not this fragment).

- **Changed or dropped assertions.** (1) The Bash `yq eval -i` mutations become parsed-copy edits (`js-yaml`), as the
  existing `model-resolve-kit.ts` `mutatedRoot` already does; `yq` is needed only for the shell oracle until the
  I2 switch and is not required by the replacements. (2) `O8` and `M8` guard a property of shell function text; they
  have no TypeScript meaning and are dropped unless the plan keeps a TypeScript hash golden (marked above).
  (3) `M7`'s `sed` rewrite of `model-resolve.sh` becomes a text mutation of the temp copy of
  `scripts/lib/model-resolve/*.ts`. (4) `sha256sum`/`sort`/`comm`/`awk` pipelines become Node. (5) The `FATAL: yq`
  exit 2 and the `ok`/`bad` counters are dropped (`node --test` reports). (6) `C9`'s `awk` windows become a parse
  of `ci/ci-capabilities.yml` and of the `component-drift` job; the thresholds 3 and 4 are kept. (7) The in-suite
  comment that `M10` is asserted by `build-components-cleanup.test.ts` is kept in the new real-tree file header.
  (8) The suite runs `build-components.sh`; replacements run `node scripts/build-components.ts` directly (the shim
  is row J4, its argv forwarding is already proved by `build-components-shim.test.ts`). Nothing else dropped.
- **Sourced-library cases (parent R13 exception).** In-process `. scripts/lib/model-resolve.sh`: every case that
  calls `resolve_agent` or reads `DIAG_LINES` (C1, C3-C7, C13(i), the rules, R24/D16, (g)(5)/(g)(6), D12/D17, C10, M1-M6,
  O1-O7, O9, O11, M11, O12), `M7` (sources the mutated copy), `O8`/`M8` (hash the library text). They retire with
  `model-resolve.sh` in the I2 library-retirement pull request, mapped above to TypeScript tests of
  `scripts/lib/model-resolve/**` that already exist (0262 requirement 17). No bash-spawning POSIX-only port is
  planned, so there is NO 'Retires with scripts/lib/common.sh (row J4)' marker for this suite; the one sourced
  library is `model-resolve.sh`, owned by I2. `VERIFY AT PLAN TIME`: if the I2 switch retains `model-resolve.sh` until J4
  (0262 requirement 16 mentions J4 for the shim only), re-mark the shell-oracle legs `Retires with
  scripts/lib/common.sh (row J4)` and keep a POSIX-only bash-spawning port.

  `CREWRIG_REPO_UNDER_TEST`, default the checkout), run in a sandbox that copies git-tracked files plus a REAL
  copy of `js-yaml` and `argparse`. (1) `scripts/lib/model-resolve/` rule (g)(0) condition (i) (the
  `profile_declares_item != true -> return` gate): delete -> rule (b) test (`-degraded`) red, a second record

  body gains a no-op: only if the hash guard is kept (twin of M8). (4) `scripts/lib/build-components/**` append of the guidance

Both suites below are oracle suites of `specs/0262-model-ci-tooling-typescript.md` requirement 26: they stay
unchanged until the I2 switch merges and pass against the TypeScript entries through the shims. Their migration
is a J2 pull request opened AFTER that switch. Until then the byte-for-byte proof is the differential proof of
0262 requirement 21 (`scripts/tests/model-ci-tooling-differential.test.ts`, 146 lines, 6 tests, helper
`scripts/tests/lib/bash-suite-differential.ts`): it runs these two Bash suites UNCHANGED in a sandbox whose
script is a wrapper running both implementations. That harness tests only its own controls and scratch guards,
never an assertion of the two suites, so no assertion below is already covered by an existing `node --test`
file (overlap check by grep over `scripts/tests/*.test.ts`: no hit for `check-ci-parity` or
`check-spec-id-reserved` outside the differential and wiring tests). The twins are
`scripts/check-ci-parity.ts` and `scripts/check-spec-id-reserved.ts` (names per 0262 requirement 1; helper
modules under `scripts/lib/` are named by the I2 plan: *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME* against the merged switch).
Ticket notes carried from I1 (#1338), to keep: R4t deletes the `Install tesseract` step of `figure-labels`
(not `Install yq` of `check-feedback-routing`); P6 and S12 mutate `figure-labels` `requires.tools` (fixture-only
edit: no capability declares `python3` any more); SC1 and SC2 select the `release-notes` capability (suite
`test-monorepo-release-notes.sh`). The group is not sourced-library use: neither suite sources a `scripts/lib/*.sh`
file (R13 exception does not apply; `check-spec-id-reserved.sh` sources `scripts/lib/base-ref-resolve.sh` itself,
and that library is covered by `scripts/tests/governance-base-ref.test.ts`, 189 lines, 15 tests, which this
migration neither duplicates nor changes).

##### `scripts/tests/test-reserve-spec-id.sh` (903 lines)

Row I2 (#1339, spec 0262 and deltas 01-04) owns the tool; this suite is the oracle suite of spec 0262 requirement 26, which runs unchanged through the forwarding shim `scripts/reserve-spec-id.sh` after the I2 switch. Row J2 migrates it only after that switch, so the replacement drives the TypeScript entry `scripts/reserve-spec-id.ts` (pull request #1680 "Add the spec-id twins", not merged at authoring time: every name below that comes from it is *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME* against the merged tree). Behaviour is frozen: the replacement asserts the exit contract and message content of the Bash suite, nothing new.

- **Replacement suite.** Groups in file order: GA = Cases 1-4 (L412-478, exit contract), GB = Cases 5-6 (L480-506, allocated set), GC = Cases 7-10 (L508-597, atomicity), GD = Cases 11-17 (L599-679, maintainer mode and the two corpora), GE = Cases 18-23 (L681-759, carrier setting), GR = Cases 24-27 (L761-814, requirement 12 refusals), GF = Cases 28-32 (L816-896, per-issue reuse, spec 0112 delta-02). Overlap check done (`ls scripts/tests`, grep `reserve-spec-id|spec-id-carrier`): no existing `scripts/tests/*.test.ts` on `main` mentions the tool. On the open PR #1680: `model-ci-tooling-reserve-spec-id.test.ts` (178 lines) is a RELATIVE differential (shell leg versus TypeScript leg on two identically seeded scratch remotes, equal stdout, stderr, status and ref object names; the shell leg is skipped on win32) with only three absolute assertions (`0013` for the union over primary carrier `0010`, tags carrier `0012`, org ref `0099`, org spec `0099`, delta spec; statuses `[0,0,1]`, `[3,3,3]`, `[1...]`); `model-ci-tooling-spec-id-suites.test.ts` (92 lines) runs THIS Bash suite through the differential wrapper (the oracle itself); `windows-model-ci-tooling-spec-id.test.ts` (153) is the Windows smoke; `model-ci-tooling-differential-safety.test.ts` (171) guards the helpers. After the switch the differential's shell leg is the shim, so it compares the TypeScript entry to itself and only its absolute assertions keep value; it disappears with the shim at J4. Not covered by anything: the exact stdout shape (line 1 id, line 2 `unsecured-id: true`) for Cases 2/3/21, the identifying stderr content (flag, value, ticket `#930`, file name), the hidden-namespace degrade (Case 21, `transfer.hideRefs`), the genuine concurrent races (Cases 7, 8, 32, sleeping `pre-receive` hook), the parentless-object property and its premise (Case 10), the `crewrig|origin|first remote` fallback arm (Case 23), reuse across carriers and per issue (Cases 28-31), and the `specs/org/` exclusion on its own (Case 6). Those are what the new files add; Cases 5-6 are ported although the differential's `0013` test is close to them, because that test leaves with J4.

Each test file stays under 300 lines; the fixture helper is the only file under `scripts/tests/lib/`. `reserve-spec-id-fixture.ts` MAY instead extend `lib/spec-id-twins.ts` (PR #1680, `makeSide`, `runTool`, `baseEnv`) if the plan finds the two-remote fixture fits; the Bash fixture needs BOTH remotes `crewrig` and `origin` and a `use_single_remote` rewrite, which `makeSide` (one remote) does not give: *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME* (default: separate helper, shared `git` and containment guard only).

**Fixture and safety rules (binding on the plan).**

- Every bare remote, clone, HOME and hook lives under one `fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "reserve-spec-id-"))`, removed in `after` with `fs.rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true })` on that path only. The real `origin`, the real `refs/spec-ids/*` and `crewrig/crewrig` are never contacted: the work clone is built with `git init`, not cloned from the checkout; remote URLs are the literal `<root>/<case>/bare.git` strings built by the helper from the mkdtemp root, never taken from env or argv; `assertRemotesUnderScratch(work, root)` (`lib/scratch-remote.ts` L81, resolves with `fs.realpathSync`) runs after every remote add or `set-url` and before every tool call, and a test proves the guard throws on a remote outside the root (spec 0262 requirement 22 pattern). Case 3's absent remote is `<root>/c3/absent.git` (a path under the root that does not exist), never an unresolvable URL.
- `CREWRIG_REPO_UNDER_TEST` (new, optional, for the mutation runs only): resolved with `fs.realpathSync`, accepted only when it equals the checkout root or lies under `fs.realpathSync(os.tmpdir())`; otherwise the file throws before spawning anything. It selects the directory holding `scripts/reserve-spec-id.ts`. The path of the work clone is passed to the tool as `CREWRIG_REPO_DIR=<work>` (as the Bash `run_tool` does) and as `cwd`.
- Child env: copy of `process.env` with `NODE_TEST_CONTEXT` unset (otherwise `node --test` children recurse into reporter mode), `CI`, `GITHUB_ACTIONS`, `GITLAB_CI`, `BASE_REF`, every `CREWRIG_SPEC_ID_*`, `GIT_DIR`, `GIT_WORK_TREE`, `GIT_INDEX_FILE` deleted (a developer or CI value of `CI`, `BASE_REF` or `CREWRIG_SPEC_ID_CARRIER` must not leak in); `LC_ALL=C` (git's messages and `grep -E` classes must not vary, stderr is read verbatim); `HOME` and `USERPROFILE` set to a scratch dir; `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1` and NO `GIT_CONFIG_SYSTEM=<os.devNull>` (Windows git rejects that value); fixed `GIT_AUTHOR_*` and `GIT_COMMITTER_*` from `FIXED_GIT_ENV` (`lib/scratch-remote.ts` L11) plus `user.email`, `user.name`, `commit.gpgsign=false` set in the work clone as the Bash `new_fixture` does; a stub `gh` first on PATH (generated at runtime by `writeStubGh`, `lib/scratch-remote.ts` L72) that fails the test if called (the tool must not use a forge CLI). Per-call `timeout` of 60 s and `maxBuffer`; the race files use 30 s.
- Entry: `process.execPath --disable-warning=MODULE_TYPELESS_PACKAGE_JSON <repo>/scripts/reserve-spec-id.ts <args>` (the shape of `scripts/tests/lib/spec-id-twins.ts` `runTool`). One test per file ALSO runs the shim `bash <repo>/scripts/reserve-spec-id.sh --issue <n>` on POSIX to prove the shim forwards status and stdout (Case 1 shape); it duplicates the shim contract test of spec 0259, so keep it to one test or drop it: *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME*.
- Fixtures: the sleeping hook is generated at runtime into `<bare>/hooks/pre-receive` (mode `0o755`, `#!/bin/sh\nsleep <n>\nexit 0\n`), so no `.sh` fixture is tracked and nothing enters `ci/shell-allowlist.txt` or the ratchet; if the plan prefers a tracked fixture it MUST carry the `.golden` suffix. No tracked golden is needed.

- **Assertion mapping.** Case labels are those of the Bash suite (`Case N`); every `expect*` of a case is listed in the last column.

| label | behaviour asserted | replacement::test intent | MUST survive |
|---|---|---|---|

No other group exists beyond Cases 1-32 (the Bash suite prints one `PASS` per `expect*`, 78 at the last recorded count in the header comment L116; the plan records the current count by running the suite at the base commit and checks the replacement has at least one `assert` per `expect*` row above: the exact count is recorded at plan time, *VERIFY AT PLAN TIME*).

Hook sleep stays 2 s (measured for exactly one winner on git 2.55.0, comment L235-239); the fixture asserts the hook is present before the overlap starts so a missing hook cannot turn Cases 8 and 32 into a vacuous pass.

### Decision record

#### Pull request grouping

Nine pull requests, named P1 to P9 so that they are not confused with the parent rows A to J, and so that each stays reviewable (about 1.3 to 3.2 thousand removed Bash lines) and so that the
long CI jobs overlap. They open from the same release tip in parallel worktrees, are independent of one another
except for the shared helpers named in the sections (a helper is created by the first pull request that needs it and
imported by later ones only after it has merged), and merge one at a time at the coordinator's request. Because
`ci/ci-capabilities.yml` and the generated files are shared by every row of the wave, each pull request regenerates
them from the current release tip just before its merge request. Pull requests P1 to P7 do not depend on I2 and open
first; P8 and P9 (requirement 22) open only after the I2 switch pull request is merged and #1339 is closed, and only
after the coordinator agrees.

| PR | Branch | Suites | Bash lines removed |
|---|---|---|---:|
| P1 | `test/1343-build-core` | `test-agent-profile-migration`, `test-artifact-build-install-scope`, `test-assembly-verification`, `test-pr-logbook-label-scope`, `scripts/test-build-components.sh`, `test-build-claude-plugin-agents-glob`, `test-extract-frontmatter`, `test-gemini-agent-frontmatter` | about 1.4 k |
| P2 | `test/1343-extension-builders` | `test-build-extension`, `test-migrate-extension`, `test-create-extension-combinations`, `test-extension-render-conformance` | about 2.0 k |
| P3 | `test/1343-release-packaging` | `test-build-docs-index`, `test-release-package-extension`, `test-monorepo-release`, `-engine`, `-notes`, `generate-github-golden.sh` | about 1.9 k |
| P4 | `test/1343-governance-checks-a` | seven `test-check-*` of the first governance fragment (claude-agent-layout, component-metadata-keys, core-paths, extension-hook-map, -hook-tokens, -manifest-version, -pivot) | about 1.9 k |
| P5 | `test/1343-governance-checks-b` | seven `test-check-*` of the second fragment (extension-provenance, -version-bump, feedback-routing, figure-labels, markdown-links, no-machine-paths, skill-versions) | about 1.9 k |
| P6 | `test/1343-governance-guards-spec-linter` | `test-check-gemini-overlay-enrollment`, `-test-strays`, `-test-wiring`, `test-spec-linter` | about 2.9 k |
| P7 | `test/1343-harness-curator` | `artifacts/library/skills/harness-curator/scripts/test.sh` | about 1.3 k |
| P8 | `test/1343-model-ci-checks` | `test-build-ci`, `test-check-agent-profiles`, `test-check-model-mappings`, `test-model-resolution` (gated by requirement 22) | about 2.9 k |
| P9 | `chore/1343-ci-parity-spec-id` | `test-check-ci-parity`, `test-check-spec-id-reserved`, `test-reserve-spec-id` (gated by requirement 22); flips this spec and its deltas to `implemented`; documentation sweep | about 2.8 k |

`test-spec-linter.sh` has no migration row and a script that stays JavaScript, so it is not gated; it rides with P6
because both are guard-like and P6 is the smallest pull request that can carry it.

#### Rejected alternatives

- **One pull request per suite (forty).** Rejected: the CI restart after each merge into the release branch costs 25 to
  35 minutes per queued pull request, and the seat passes multiply.
- **One pull request for the whole row.** Rejected: about 20 thousand removed lines in one diff cannot be reviewed against the mapping.
- **Migrate the I2 suites now against the shell oracle.** Rejected by the coordinator's oracle rule: the I2 TypeScript twins
  are not the shipped implementation until the switch pull request merges.
- **Keep `test-monorepo-release-notes.sh` as Bash until J4.** Rejected: spec 0257 requirement 21 freezes its assertions only until
  this row and the suite asserts no shell-library behaviour.
- **Port the nested `node --test` wrappers and the `fzf` stubs one for one.** Rejected: they assert the Bash file's own helpers or a
  dead prompt, not the product.

## Scenarios

**Scenario:** A broken builder turns the new suite red

Given a replacement suite of a build or extension-builder suite and a temporary copy of the implementation in which a step
is mutated
When the replacement suite runs against the mutated copy
Then at least the test that the Bash suite named for that step fails, and the unmutated copy passes the whole suite.

**Scenario:** A governance check keeps its twin honest

Given a replacement of a `test-check-*.sh` suite
When a mutant is applied to the TypeScript twin of the check and to its forwarding shim
Then the replacement turns red for each mutant that turned the Bash suite red.

**Scenario:** The release goldens regenerate identically

Given the TypeScript golden generator
When it runs in an Ubuntu 24.04 image with Node under `/usr/local/bin` and again in one with Node under
`/opt/hostedtoolcache/node/<version>/x64/bin`
Then `diff -r` of the two outputs and of the committed goldens is empty, and no publish was attempted.

**Scenario:** The harness-curator proofs never touch real state

Given the replacement of the harness-curator suite
When it runs with a scratch palace, a scratch home and a stub `gh`
Then the real MemPalace, `~/.mempalace` and GitHub are untouched and the stub logs no `issue create`.

**Scenario:** A sourced-library suite is retired with a substitute

Given the deletion of a Bash suite that sourced a shell library
When each assertion of the suite is looked up in its mapping
Then it names either an existing black-box test of the TypeScript twin or a bash-spawning port marked
`Retires with scripts/lib/common.sh (row J4)`.

**Scenario:** An I2 suite waits for its switch

Given the I2 switch pull request is not merged
When this row's pull requests are planned
Then no suite of requirement 22 has started, and the coordinator is asked before each of P8 and P9 opens.

**Scenario:** A deleted suite leaves the repository consistent

Given a pull request that replaces a Bash suite
When `check-test-wiring`, `check-test-strays`, the shell ratchet and the CI generator run
Then they pass, `ci/shell-allowlist.txt` no longer lists the suite and no workflow, document or allowlist of this row names it.

**Scenario:** A defect is found while porting

Given a replacement test that exposes a behaviour of a script that looks wrong
When the developer reaches that test
Then the behaviour is reported to the coordinator, the test asserts the current behaviour with a comment naming the report,
and the script is unchanged.

## Out of scope

- The Bash tests of rows J1a, J1b and J3, and `test-check-bash32-portability.sh` and `test-check-pipefail-grep.sh` (J4), and any edit to them.
- Retiring `scripts/lib/common.sh` and the other shell libraries, and the final shim removal (row J4).
- Changing the behaviour of a script, a hook, a fixture format or a diagnostic line.
- The Windows timing-budget calibration (#1509) and the other follow-ups.
- A change to `package-lock.json`.
- Any new dependency.

## Open questions

None. The questions raised at authoring time are closed on the logbook issue #1343 (closure comment before approval) and recorded here:

- The sourced-library reading follows spec 0263 requirement 6, already confirmed with the coordinator there.
- `test-monorepo-release-notes.sh` migrates in this row (see the decision record); spec 0257 requirement 21 froze it only until J2.
- Spec 0145 names `test-check-markdown-links.sh` in its requirement 6. The normative text of a merged spec is immutable and the behaviour of the suite is carried by its replacement, so the reference is accepted drift: the pull request that deletes the suite records it in its body, and no delta-spec of 0145 is opened.
- The per-suite `VERIFY AT PLAN TIME` items are not open questions of this spec: requirement 24 makes the PLAN carry each one as a checklist item that gates the pull request it belongs to.
